# List 29

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conflagration</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːnfləˈɡreɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大火；（大规模）冲突，战争</td><td>a global conflagration<br>全球性战争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A minor border dispute soon escalated into a conflagration that engulfed the whole region.  一场小小的边界争端很快升级为吞没整个地区的战火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conflict</td><td>/ˈkɑːnflɪkt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>冲突，矛盾；抵触</td><td>a conflict of interest<br>利益冲突</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge withdrew because of a possible conflict of interest.  法官因可能存在利益冲突而回避。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 war</td><td>/ˈwɔːr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>冲突，斗争；战争</td><td>a price war<br>价格战</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A price war between the two airlines cut fares sharply.  两家航空公司的价格战大幅压低了票价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 warfare</td><td>/ˈwɔːrˌfer/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>战争；交战；冲突</td><td>guerrilla warfare<br>游击战</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Modern warfare relies on drones and satellites far more than on massed infantry charges.  现代战争更多依赖无人机和卫星，而非大规模步兵冲锋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 peace</td><td>/ˈpiːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和平，和睦；平静</td><td>live in peace<br>和睦相处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two villages have lived in peace for over a century.  这两个村庄和睦相处已逾一个世纪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conflate</b></td><td>/kənˈfleɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>合并，混为一谈</td><td>conflate two separate issues<br>把两个问题混为一谈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics often conflate two separate issues and confuse the public.  批评者常把两个不同的问题混为一谈，令公众困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amalgamate</td><td>/əˈmælɡəmeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>合并，混合</td><td>amalgamate two companies<br>合并两家公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two small colleges amalgamated to cut administrative costs.  两所小学院合并以削减行政成本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 combine</td><td>/kəmˈbaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，联合</td><td>combine two elements<br>结合两种元素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The recipe combines sweet and sour flavors very successfully.  这道菜谱把甜味和酸味结合得很成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 composite</td><td>/kəmˈpɑːzət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>合成的；合成物</td><td>a composite picture<br>合成的图像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The artist built a composite portrait from four separate photographs.  艺术家用四张照片合成了一幅肖像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fuse</td><td>/ˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融合，熔合</td><td>fuse two traditions<br>融合两种传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her music fuses jazz rhythms with traditional folk melodies.  她的音乐把爵士节奏与传统民谣旋律融为一体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 homogenize</td><td>/həˈmɑːdʒənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使统一化</td><td>homogenize the curriculum<br>使课程统一化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 National testing tends to homogenize teaching across very different schools.  全国统考往往使差异很大的学校的教学趋于统一。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 immingle</del></td><td>/ɪˈmɪŋɡəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>混合，融入</td><td>immingle with the crowd<br>混入人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voices from the street immingled with the music inside the hall.  街上的声音与厅内的音乐混在了一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incorporate</td><td>/ɪnˈkɔːrpəreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>包含，纳入；体现</td><td>incorporate new ideas<br>纳入新想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The revised design incorporates suggestions from every member of the team.  修订后的设计纳入了团队每位成员的建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 integrate</td><td>/ˈɪntəˌɡreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>整合，使成一体</td><td>integrate into the system<br>融入体系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school works hard to integrate new arrivals into class life.  学校努力让新来者融入班级生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 interfuse</del></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈfjuz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使混合，交融</td><td>interfuse the two styles<br>交融两种风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The composer interfuses folk melodies with modern harmony.  作曲家把民间旋律与现代和声交融在一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meld</td><td>/ˈmeld/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融合，合并</td><td>meld into one<br>融为一体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The choir's voices melded into a single warm sound.  合唱团的声音融合成一种温暖的声响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 merge</td><td>/ˈmɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>合并，融合</td><td>merge two companies<br>合并两家公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two banks merged last year to cut operating costs.  这两家银行去年合并以削减运营成本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mingle</td><td>/ˈmɪŋɡl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>混合；与人交往</td><td>mingle with the crowd<br>混入人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Smoke mingled with fog above the crowded harbor.  烟雾与雾气在拥挤的港口上方交织。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mix</td><td>/ˈmɪks/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>混合，掺和</td><td>mix the ingredients<br>把配料混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mix the flour and water until the dough becomes smooth.  把面粉和水混合，直到面团变得光滑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disunite</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsjuˈnaɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分裂，使不团结</td><td>disunite the party<br>使党派分裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax proposal disunited a coalition that had held for years.  这项税收提案使维持多年的联盟分裂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 divide</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分开，划分；分歧</td><td>divide into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher divided the class into four groups of five.  老师把班级分成四个五人小组。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 part</td><td>/ˈpɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>part the heavy curtains<br>分开厚重的窗帘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She parted the heavy curtains and let the morning light in.  她分开厚重的窗帘，让晨光照进来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sunder</td><td>/ˈsʌndər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，割断</td><td>sunder the alliance<br>使联盟破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bitter dispute sundered an alliance that had lasted decades.  这场激烈的争端割断了维持数十年的联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conform</b></td><td>/kənˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使协调；遵照，遵从</td><td>conform to the standard<br>符合标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All new buildings must conform to strict safety codes.  所有新建筑都必须符合严格的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accommodate</td><td>/əˈkɑːmədeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使适应；容纳；迁就</td><td>accommodate different needs<br>满足不同需求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The schedule was changed to accommodate everyone's travel plans.  日程被改动以适应每个人的出行计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attune</td><td>/əˈtuːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使协调，使和谐</td><td>attune oneself to<br>使自己适应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers are attuned to the moods of their students.  好老师能敏锐感知学生的情绪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conciliate</td><td>/kənˈsɪlieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；调解</td><td>conciliate angry customers<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager tried to conciliate the angry customers with refunds.  经理试图用退款来安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coordinate</td><td>/koʊˈɔːrdɪneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>协调，使配合</td><td>coordinate the relief effort<br>协调救援工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two agencies coordinated the rescue effort after the flood.  洪水过后两个机构协调了救援工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 key</td><td>/ˈkiː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>关键的；钥匙</td><td>a key factor<br>关键因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trust is the key element in any long working relationship.  信任是任何长期工作关系中的关键要素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reconcile</td><td>/ˈrekənsaɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使和解，协调</td><td>reconcile conflicting views<br>调和相互冲突的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is hard to reconcile these two versions of the story.  很难把这个故事的两种说法协调一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adhere</td><td>/ədˈhɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>服从，遵守</td><td>adhere to the rules<br>遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All staff must adhere to the safety procedures without exception.  全体员工必须无例外地遵守安全规程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comply</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>遵从，服从</td><td>comply with the rules<br>遵守规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All drivers must comply with the new speed limits.  所有司机都必须遵守新的限速规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 follow</td><td>/ˈfɑːloʊ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遵循，跟随</td><td>follow the rules<br>遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 If you follow the instructions exactly, nothing can go wrong.  只要严格按说明操作，就不会出错。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 goose-step</td><td>/ˈɡuːs stɛp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>正步走；盲目服从</td><td>goose-step across the square<br>正步走过广场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soldiers goose-step past the reviewing stand every October.  士兵们每年十月都正步走过检阅台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mind</td><td>/ˈmaɪnd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>留意，注意；介意</td><td>mind the step<br>留意台阶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please mind the gap between the train and the platform.  请注意列车与站台之间的空隙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 observe</td><td>/əbˈzɜːrv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>遵守；观察</td><td>observe the law<br>遵守法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All visitors are expected to observe the museum's rules.  所有参观者都应遵守博物馆的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disharmonize</del></td><td>/dɪsˈhɑrmənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不协调，使失和</td><td>disharmonize a group<br>使团体失和</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless remarks can disharmonize an otherwise united team.  不慎的言论会使原本团结的团队失和。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defy</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>蔑视，公然违抗</td><td>defy an order<br>违抗命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of workers defied the ban and gathered in the main square.  数千名工人公然违抗禁令，聚集在中心广场上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disobey</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈbeɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>不服从，违抗</td><td>disobey an order<br>违抗命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soldier was punished for choosing to disobey a direct order.  这名士兵因选择违抗直接命令而受到处罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebel</td><td>/rɪˈbel/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>反抗，造反；反叛者</td><td>rebel against authority<br>反抗权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students rebelled against the strict new curfew last spring.  学生们去年春天反抗严苛的新宵禁令。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conjure</b></td><td>/kənˈdʒʊr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恳求，央求；使浮现于脑海</td><td>conjure him to stay<br>恳求他留下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She conjured him to stay until the doctor finally arrived.  她恳求他留下，直到医生终于赶来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appeal</td><td>/əˈpiːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>上诉；恳求，呼吁</td><td>file an appeal<br>提出上诉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lawyers will file an appeal against the verdict next week.  她的律师下周将针对判决提出上诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beseech</td><td>/bɪˈsiːtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恳求，哀求</td><td>beseech him for mercy<br>恳求他宽恕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoners beseeched the governor for mercy in a long letter.  囚犯们在一封长信中恳求州长开恩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 besiege</td><td>/bɪˈsiːdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>围攻；不断烦扰</td><td>besiege the city<br>围攻城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters besieged the minister with questions about the scandal.  记者们就丑闻不断向部长发问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entreat</td><td>/ɪnˈtriːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恳求</td><td>entreat her to return<br>恳求她回来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She entreated him to return before their mother learned of the quarrel.  她恳求他在母亲得知这场争吵之前回来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 impetrate</del></td><td>/ˈɪmpɪtreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恳求，祈求得到</td><td>impetrate divine mercy<br>祈求神的宽恕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers gathered to impetrate rain from the heavens.  村民聚集起来向上天祈求降雨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 implore</td><td>/ˌɪmˈplɔːr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恳求，哀求</td><td>implore for help<br>苦苦求助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She implored the judge to give her brother another chance.  她恳求法官再给她弟弟一次机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 importune</del></td><td>/ˌɪmpɔːrˈtuːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恳求，迫切请求</td><td>importune him for money<br>向他苦苦求钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Beggars importuned travelers for coins outside the station all evening.  乞丐整晚在车站外向旅客苦苦讨要零钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 petition</td><td>/pəˈtɪʃn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>请愿书；请求</td><td>sign a petition<br>在请愿书上签名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands signed the petition to save the old public library.  数千人签署请愿书，要求保住这座旧的公共图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plead</td><td>/ˈpliːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>恳求；辩护，申辩</td><td>plead for mercy<br>恳求宽恕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoner pleaded for mercy in a trembling, exhausted voice.  囚犯用颤抖疲惫的声音恳求宽恕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pray</td><td>/ˈpreɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>祈求，恳求</td><td>pray for mercy<br>祈求宽恕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They prayed for the safe return of the fishermen.  他们祈求渔民平安归来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solicit</td><td>/səˈlɪsɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>请求，征求；招揽</td><td>solicit donations<br>募集捐款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity solicited funds from local businesses all winter.  该慈善机构整个冬天向本地企业募捐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supplicate</td><td>/ˈsʌplɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>恳求，乞求</td><td>supplicate for forgiveness<br>乞求宽恕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoner knelt to supplicate for forgiveness before the military tribunal.  囚犯跪下，在军事法庭前乞求宽恕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conceive</td><td>/kənˈsiːv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>构想，想出；理解</td><td>conceive a bold plan<br>构想大胆的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I cannot conceive why she would refuse such a generous offer.  我无法理解她为何会拒绝这么优厚的条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dream</td><td>/ˈdriːm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>梦想，幻想；做梦</td><td>dream of fame<br>幻想成名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a child he dreamed of sailing around the world.  他小时候幻想过环球航行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 envisage</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪzɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>设想，想象</td><td>envisage a solution<br>设想一个解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody envisaged such a rapid change in the market.  没人设想过市场会有如此迅速的变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 envision</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪʒən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>想象，展望</td><td>envision the future<br>展望未来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She envisions a city where nobody needs a car.  她想象着一座无人需要汽车的城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fancy</td><td>/ˈfænsi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>喜爱，想要</td><td>fancy a cup of tea<br>想喝杯茶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do you fancy a walk along the river this evening?  今晚想不想去河边散散步？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fantasize</td><td>/ˈfæntəˌsaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>幻想，想象</td><td>fantasize about winning<br>幻想获胜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He often fantasized about quitting and moving to the coast.  他常幻想辞职搬到海边。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feature</td><td>/ˈfiːtʃər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>想象，设想；以…为特色</td><td>hard to feature it<br>难以想象那情形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I simply cannot feature him living quietly in the countryside.  我实在无法想象他在乡下安静生活的样子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ideate</del></td><td>/ˈaɪdieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>构思，形成概念</td><td>ideate a new product<br>构思一款新产品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team met weekly to ideate fresh marketing campaigns.  团队每周开会构思新的营销方案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 image</td><td>/ˈɪmədʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>想象，在心中描绘</td><td>image the scene clearly<br>清晰地想象场景</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to image the room exactly as it looked then.  试着想象那房间当时的确切模样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 picture</td><td>/ˈpɪktʃər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>想象，设想；图画</td><td>picture himself as a hero<br>想象自己是英雄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to picture the room as it looked a century ago.  试着想象这房间一百年前的样子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vision</td><td>/ˈvɪʒən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>幻象，梦幻；视力；远见</td><td>have a vision of<br>看到…的幻象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The saint claimed to have seen a vision of a burning city.  这位圣徒声称看到过一座燃烧城市的幻象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 visualize</td><td>/ˈvɪʒuəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>想象，使形象化</td><td>visualize the finished building<br>想象建成后的大楼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Athletes often visualize the race before they actually run it.  运动员常在真正比赛前先想象整个过程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>consent</b></td><td>/kənˈsent/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>give one's consent<br>表示同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children cannot travel without their parents' consent.  没有父母的同意，孩子们不能出行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allowance</td><td>/əˈlaʊəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>限额；津贴</td><td>a baggage allowance<br>行李限额</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each passenger has a baggage allowance of two small bags.  每位乘客的行李限额是两个小包。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 approval</td><td>/əˈpruːvəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>批准，认可；赞成</td><td>seek her approval<br>征求她的同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan needs approval from the board before work begins.  该计划开工前需要董事会批准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authorization</td><td>/ˌɔːθərəˈzeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>授权，批准</td><td>official authorization<br>正式授权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one may enter the lab without written authorization.  未经书面授权，任何人不得进入实验室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clearance</td><td>/ˈklɪrəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>批准，许可</td><td>security clearance for staff<br>员工的安全许可</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pilot waited for clearance before entering the runway.  飞行员在进入跑道前等待许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concurrence</td><td>/kənˈkɜːrəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>同意，一致；同时发生</td><td>with the concurrence of<br>在…同意之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan proceeded with the concurrence of all board members.  该计划在全体董事同意之下推进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 granting</td><td>/ˈɡræntɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>准予，授予</td><td>the granting of permission<br>许可的授予</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The granting of the visa took nearly three months.  签证的批准花了将近三个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 license</td><td>/ˈlaɪsns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>许可，认可；自由</td><td>a license to practice<br>执业许可证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally obtained a license to practice medicine in three different states.  她终于取得了在三个不同州行医的执业许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sanction</td><td>/ˈsæŋkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批准，同意，认可</td><td>sanction the new policy<br>批准这项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board refused to sanction the plan without a detailed budget.  董事会拒绝在没有详细预算的情况下批准该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 warrant</td><td>/ˈwɔːrənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保证，担保；使有正当理由</td><td>warrant the quality<br>保证质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory warrants the quality of every tool it sells abroad.  这家工厂为其出口的每件工具的质量提供保证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acquiesce</td><td>/ˌækwiˈes/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>勉强同意；默许</td><td>acquiesce in the decision<br>默许该决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She acquiesced in their plan rather than start an argument.  她默许了他们的计划，以免引起争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agree</td><td>/əˈɡriː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>同意，赞同；一致</td><td>agree to the terms<br>同意这些条款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides finally agreed to the new safety rules.  双方最终同意了新的安全规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 approve</td><td>/əˈpruːv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>批准；赞成</td><td>approve the budget<br>批准预算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee finally approved the plan after three long meetings.  委员会经过三次长会终于批准了该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assent</td><td>/əˈsent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>assent to the proposal<br>赞同该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee assented to the proposal without further debate.  委员会未经进一步辩论就同意了该提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subscribe</td><td>/səbˈskraɪb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>赞同（观点）；订阅</td><td>subscribe to a view<br>赞同某观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not subscribe to the view that money buys happiness.  我不赞同金钱能买到幸福的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 interdiction</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈdɪkʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>禁止，阻断，封锁</td><td>drug interdiction efforts<br>禁毒阻断行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Naval interdiction stopped most of the smuggling along the coast.  海军的阻断行动制止了沿岸大部分走私。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prohibition</td><td>/ˌproʊəˈbɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>禁止，禁令</td><td>a prohibition on smoking<br>禁止吸烟的规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city enforced a prohibition against building near the lake.  该市执行了禁止在湖边建房的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 proscription</td><td>/proʊˈskrɪpʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>禁止，查禁；剥夺权利</td><td>the proscription of books<br>对书籍的查禁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wartime proscription of certain newspapers lasted for several years.  战时对某些报纸的查禁持续了数年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>consign</b></td><td>/kənˈsaɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>转交，托付；把…置于（某境地）</td><td>consign goods to a buyer<br>把货物转交买主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court consigned the orphaned children to the care of their aunt.  法院把这几个孤儿转交给他们的姑妈照管。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commend</td><td>/kəˈmend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬，表扬</td><td>commend her courage<br>赞扬她的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal commended her courage in front of the whole school.  校长在全校面前赞扬了她的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commit</td><td>/kəˈmɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>交托，把…交付给</td><td>commit it to her care<br>把它交由她照管</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They committed the child to the care of her aunt.  他们把孩子交给她姑妈照顾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confide</td><td>/kənˈfaɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>委托，托付；倾诉</td><td>confide a secret to him<br>向他吐露秘密</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She confided the manuscript to her most trusted editor.  她把手稿托付给最信赖的编辑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delegate</td><td>/ˈdelɪˌɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>委派，下放（权力、任务）</td><td>delegate authority to others<br>把权力下放给别人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good leader delegates routine tasks and saves time for planning.  好的领导者会把日常事务移交出去，为规划省下时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deliver</td><td>/dɪˈlɪvər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>移交，交付</td><td>deliver the documents personally<br>亲自交付文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The courier delivered the sealed envelope to her office.  快递员把密封信封送到了她的办公室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entrust</td><td>/ɪnˈtrʌst/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>委托，托付</td><td>entrust him with the keys<br>把钥匙托付给他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They entrusted the family business to their youngest son.  他们把家族生意托付给了小儿子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hand</td><td>/ˈhænd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>递交，交给</td><td>hand over the report<br>把报告交出去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He handed the letter to his supervisor without a word.  他一言不发地把信递给了主管。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recommend</td><td>/ˌrekəˈmend/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>托付；推荐</td><td>recommend her to his care<br>把她托付给他照顾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dying man recommended his children to his brother.  临终的他把孩子托付给了弟弟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repose</td><td>/rɪˈpoʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（劳作后的）休息；平静，宁静</td><td>seek repose<br>寻求休息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the long harvest, the villagers enjoyed a few days of repose.  漫长的收割之后，村民们享受了几天的休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transfer</td><td>/trænsˈfɜːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>转移，调动；转让</td><td>transfer the funds<br>转移资金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bank will transfer the money to her account tomorrow.  银行明天会把钱转到她的账户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transmit</td><td>/trænzˈmɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>传送，发送；传播</td><td>transmit a signal<br>发送信号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The station transmits its broadcast to villages across the valley.  该电台把节目发送到山谷各处的村庄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vest</td><td>/ˈvest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>授予（权力）；背心</td><td>vest authority in<br>将权力授予</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The constitution vests final authority in the highest court.  宪法将最终权力授予最高法院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hold</td><td>/ˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>怀有，心存</td><td>hold a grudge<br>心怀怨恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He still holds a grudge against his former business partner.  他仍然对昔日的生意伙伴心怀怨恨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 keep</td><td>/ˈkiːp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遵守，坚持；保留</td><td>keep a promise<br>守约，履行承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He always keeps his promises, however inconvenient they are.  无论多不方便，他总是守约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 retain</td><td>/rɪˈteɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保留，留住</td><td>retain the original title<br>保留原名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum retained the building's original facade during the renovation.  翻修时博物馆保留了建筑的原有正面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>consternation</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːnstərˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>惊愕，惊惶</td><td>to everyone's consternation<br>令所有人惊愕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His resignation caused considerable consternation among the board members.  他的辞职让董事们相当惊愕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alarm</td><td>/əˈlɑːrm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>惊恐，恐慌</td><td>cause widespread alarm<br>引起普遍惊恐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden noise filled the whole household with alarm.  突然的响声让全家人惊恐不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apprehension</td><td>/ˌæprɪˈhenʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>忧虑，担心；逮捕</td><td>a sense of apprehension<br>忧虑感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She waited for the results with growing apprehension.  她越来越忧虑地等待结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dread</td><td>/dred/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恐惧</td><td>fill him with dread<br>使他充满恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thought of another interview filled him with dread all weekend.  一想到还要再面试一次，他整个周末都充满恐惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fear</td><td>/ˈfɪr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>恐惧，害怕</td><td>fear of failure<br>对失败的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fear of speaking in public gradually faded with practice.  他对公开演讲的恐惧随练习逐渐消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fright</td><td>/ˈfraɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>惊恐，恐惧</td><td>give someone a fright<br>让某人吓一跳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden bang gave the children a terrible fright.  突然的巨响让孩子们大惊失色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 horror</td><td>/ˈhɔːrər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恐怖，惊骇</td><td>watch in horror<br>惊骇地看着</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers watched in horror as the bridge collapsed.  村民惊骇地看着桥塌了下去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 terror</td><td>/ˈterər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>恐惧，惊骇</td><td>frozen with terror<br>吓得动弹不得</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children ran home in terror after hearing the story.  孩子们听完故事后惊恐地跑回家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trepidation</td><td>/ˌtrepɪˈdeɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恐惧，战栗</td><td>with some trepidation<br>怀着几分恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She opened the letter with obvious trepidation, fearing the worst news.  她带着明显的恐惧打开信，担心是最坏的消息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bravery</td><td>/ˈbreɪvəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>勇敢，英勇</td><td>an act of bravery<br>勇敢之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was given a medal for bravery during the flood.  她因在洪水中的勇敢而获得奖章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 courage</td><td>/ˈkɜːrədʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>勇气，胆量</td><td>summon up courage<br>鼓起勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took real courage to admit the mistake in front of everyone.  在众人面前承认错误需要真正的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 dauntlessness</del></td><td>/ˈdɔːntləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无畏，大胆</td><td>the dauntlessness of youth<br>年轻人的无畏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His dauntlessness in the storm saved the whole crew.  他在风暴中的无畏救了全体船员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fearlessness</td><td>/ˈfɪrləsnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无畏，大胆</td><td>admired for her fearlessness<br>因无畏而受敬佩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her fearlessness in the courtroom impressed even hostile judges.  她在法庭上的无畏甚至让敌意的法官动容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fortitude</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtɪˌtuːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>坚韧，刚毅</td><td>bear pain with fortitude<br>坚韧地忍受痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She bore months of treatment with quiet fortitude.  她默默坚韧地承受了数月的治疗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intrepidity</td><td>/ˌɪntrəˈpɪdəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>勇敢，无畏</td><td>show great intrepidity<br>表现出极大勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pilot's intrepidity saved everyone aboard that night.  那晚飞行员的无畏救了机上所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 stoutness</del></td><td>/ˈstaʊtnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刚毅，勇敢坚定</td><td>stoutness of heart<br>心志刚毅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers praised his stoutness during the long, hopeless siege.  村民赞扬他在漫长绝望的围城中的刚毅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 valor</td><td>/ˈvælər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>英勇，勇猛</td><td>an award for valor<br>英勇奖章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He received a medal for valor after rescuing two trapped miners.  他救出两名被困矿工后获得英勇勋章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contemplate</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːntəmpleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>沉思，仔细思索</td><td>contemplate the consequences<br>仔细思索后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sat by the window and contemplated the few choices left to her.  她坐在窗边，仔细思索留给自己的几个选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cogitate</del></td><td>/ˈkɑːdʒɪˌteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>认真思考，仔细考虑</td><td>cogitate on the problem<br>仔细思考这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and cogitated on his next move.  他坐在窗边，仔细思量下一步该怎么走。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consider</td><td>/kənˈsɪdər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>仔细考虑，斟酌</td><td>consider the options carefully<br>仔细斟酌各种选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She considered the options carefully before signing the lease.  她在签租约前仔细斟酌了各种选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deliberate</td><td>/dɪˈlɪbərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的，故意的</td><td>a deliberate attempt<br>故意的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was deliberate, designed to weaken our position.  这次延误是故意的，目的是削弱我们的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meditate</td><td>/ˈmedɪteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>思索，沉思</td><td>meditate on the problem<br>沉思这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and meditated on the day's events.  他坐在窗边沉思白天发生的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mull</td><td>/ˈmʌl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>反复思考，仔细琢磨</td><td>mull over the offer<br>反复考虑这个提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He mulled over the decision for several sleepless nights.  他为这个决定辗转思考了好几个夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 perpend</del></td><td>/pərˈpɛnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>仔细考虑，斟酌</td><td>perpend the evidence<br>仔细斟酌证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge asked the jury to perpend the evidence overnight.  法官要陪审团用一夜时间仔细斟酌证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pore</td><td>/pɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>钻研；仔细阅读；凝视</td><td>pore over old letters<br>仔细研读旧信件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pored over the old letters for hours without saying anything.  她埋头细读那些旧信数小时，一言不发。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revolve</td><td>/rɪˈvɑːlv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反复思量，仔细考虑</td><td>revolve the problem in mind<br>在心里反复琢磨问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She revolved the strange remark in her mind all evening.  她整晚都在心里反复琢磨那句奇怪的话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruminate</td><td>/ˈruːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>沉思；反复琢磨；反刍</td><td>ruminate on the past<br>反复思量往事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and ruminated on the past.  他坐在窗边，反复回想往事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 study</td><td>/ˈstʌdi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>学习，研究</td><td>study hard for exams<br>为考试用功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She studies medieval history at a university in Scotland.  她在苏格兰一所大学研究中世纪史。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weigh</td><td>/ˈweɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>权衡，仔细考虑</td><td>weigh the risks<br>权衡各种风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They weighed the risks for several hours before agreeing.  他们权衡风险数小时后才达成一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wrestle</td><td>/ˈresəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>全力思索，苦苦应对</td><td>wrestle with the question<br>苦苦思索这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Philosophers still wrestle with the nature of consciousness.  哲学家们仍在苦苦思索意识的本质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disregard</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>漠视，不关注；缺乏兴趣，缺乏关心</td><td>total disregard for safety<br>完全不顾安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their total disregard for safety rules eventually caused a serious accident.  他们完全不顾安全规定，最终造成了一起严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignore</td><td>/ˌɪɡˈnɔːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视，不理</td><td>ignore the warning<br>忽视警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain ignored the warning and sailed into the storm.  船长忽视了警告，驶入了风暴之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 overlook</td><td>/ˈoʊvərˌlʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视；宽恕；俯视</td><td>overlook a minor error<br>宽容小错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher chose to overlook the boy's late arrival.  老师决定不追究那男孩的迟到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contempt</b></td><td>/kənˈtempt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>蔑视，鄙视</td><td>treat with open contempt<br>公开表示蔑视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He treated their careful proposal with open and unmistakable contempt.  他对他们精心的提案表现出公开而明显的蔑视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 contemptuousness</del></td><td>/kənˈtɛmptʃuəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻蔑，鄙视</td><td>the contemptuousness of his tone<br>他语气中的轻蔑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contemptuousness in her reply silenced the entire table.  她回答中的轻蔑让整桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 despisement</del></td><td>/dɪˈspaɪzmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>鄙视，蔑视</td><td>open despisement of rivals<br>对对手的公开鄙视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His despisement of cheap sentiment shaped all his later poems.  他对廉价感伤的鄙视塑造了他后期所有的诗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 despite</td><td>/dɪˈspaɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>恶意，轻蔑</td><td>act out of despite<br>出于恶意行事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He damaged the painting out of pure despite for its owner.  他纯出恶意，损坏了那幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 despitefulness</del></td><td>/dɪˈspaɪtfəlnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恶意，怀恨</td><td>sheer despitefulness toward her<br>对她的纯粹恶意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The despitefulness of the anonymous letters shocked the small town.  那些匿名信的恶意震动了这个小镇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disdain</td><td>/dɪsˈdeɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>轻视，鄙视</td><td>disdain to answer<br>不屑于回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She disdained the flattery of her ambitious colleagues.  她鄙视那些野心勃勃的同事的奉承。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misprision</td><td>/mɪsˈprɪʒən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>知情不报；失职</td><td>misprision of a felony<br>对重罪知情不报</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with misprision of a felony he witnessed.  他因对目睹的重罪知情不报而被起诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scorn</td><td>/skɔːrn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>轻蔑，鄙视，不屑</td><td>scorn an offer<br>不屑接受提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He scorned the idea of asking his parents for money.  他不屑于向父母要钱这个想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 admiration</td><td>/ˌædməˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>钦佩，赞赏</td><td>great admiration for<br>对……极为钦佩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She earned the admiration of everyone who worked with her.  她赢得了每一位共事者的钦佩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 esteem</td><td>/ɪˈstiːm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>尊重，敬重</td><td>hold in high esteem<br>十分敬重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her colleagues esteem her for her honesty and courage.  同事们因她的诚实与勇气而敬重她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 estimation</td><td>/ˌestəˈmeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>尊重，评价；估计</td><td>rise in my estimation<br>在我心中评价提高</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her honesty raised her greatly in the estimation of her colleagues.  她的诚实大大提升了同事对她的评价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 regard</td><td>/rɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>关注，重视；看待</td><td>have regard for others<br>顾及他人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drives fast with little regard for other people.  他开车很快，几乎不顾及他人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 respect</td><td>/rɪˈspekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>尊敬，敬重；方面</td><td>show respect for<br>对……表示尊敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students should learn to respect opinions that differ from their own.  学生应学会尊重与自己不同的意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reverence</td><td>/ˈrevərəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>崇敬，尊崇</td><td>deep reverence for nature<br>对自然的深切崇敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old scholar is treated with reverence by his many students.  这位老学者受到众多学生的崇敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 veneration</td><td>/ˌvenəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>崇敬，敬仰</td><td>an object of veneration<br>崇敬的对象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ancestors are held in veneration throughout much of East Asia.  在东亚许多地区，祖先受到崇敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contend</b></td><td>/kənˈtend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>声称，主张；争夺</td><td>contend that the claim is false<br>声称该说法不实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer contended that the evidence had been obtained illegally.  律师声称这些证据是非法取得的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 battle</td><td>/ˈbætəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>斗争，交战；战斗</td><td>a legal battle<br>一场法律争斗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms are locked in a long legal battle.  两家公司陷入一场漫长的法律争斗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fight</td><td>/ˈfaɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>对抗，斗争；打斗</td><td>fight the proposal<br>反对这项提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The residents will fight the proposal to widen the old highway.  居民们将反对拓宽这条旧公路的提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 race</td><td>/ˈreɪs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>疾走，飞奔；比赛</td><td>race to the finish<br>冲向终点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He raced across the field to warn the others.  他飞奔穿过田野去警告其他人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rival</td><td>/ˈraɪvəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>对手，竞争者</td><td>a bitter rival<br>势不两立的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms have been rivals in this market for decades.  这两家公司在该市场竞争了数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vie</td><td>/ˈvaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>竞争，争夺</td><td>vie for first place<br>争夺第一名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two young pianists vied for the final prize that evening.  那晚两位年轻钢琴家争夺最后的大奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affirm</td><td>/əˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>肯定，断言</td><td>affirm one's belief<br>坚定表明信念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness affirmed that she had seen the blue car.  证人确认她看见了那辆蓝色汽车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allege</td><td>/əˈledʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>声称，指称（未经证实）</td><td>allege serious misconduct<br>指称严重不当行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prosecutors allege that the manager hid millions from investors.  检方指称该经理向投资者隐匿了数百万资金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assert</td><td>/əˈsɜːrt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>断言，声称；维护</td><td>assert one's innocence<br>声称自己无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defendant continued to assert his innocence throughout the trial.  被告在整个审判过程中不断声称自己无罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aver</td><td>/əˈvɜːr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>声称为真</td><td>aver that it is true<br>断言此事为真</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness averred that he had never met the defendant before.  证人声明他此前从未见过被告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 avouch</del></td><td>/əˈvaʊtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>断言，保证，证实</td><td>avouch the truth of it<br>保证其真实性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two witnesses avouched the accuracy of the merchant's account.  两名证人证实了这位商人陈述的准确性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avow</td><td>/əˈvaʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开承认，坦率声明</td><td>avow one's beliefs openly<br>公开表明信念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He avowed his support for the strike in front of reporters.  他在记者面前公开表明支持这次罢工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 declare</td><td>/dɪˈkler/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>宣布，声明，断言</td><td>declare one's intentions<br>表明自己的意图</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge declared the earlier verdict invalid and ordered a retrial.  法官宣布先前判决无效并下令重审。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insist</td><td>/ɪnˈsɪst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>坚持，坚决主张</td><td>insist on an apology<br>坚持要求道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She insisted that the money had been returned last week.  她坚称那笔钱上周已经归还。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maintain</td><td>/meɪnˈteɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>坚持主张；维持，保养</td><td>maintain his innocence<br>坚称自己无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He continued to maintain that he had never seen the document.  他一直坚称自己从未见过那份文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profess</td><td>/prəˈfes/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>声称，假称</td><td>profess ignorance<br>声称不知情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He professed great sympathy but offered no actual help.  他声称非常同情，却没提供实际帮助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 protest</td><td>/ˈproʊˌtest/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>抗议，反对</td><td>register a formal protest<br>提出正式抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ambassador registered a formal protest with the foreign ministry.  大使向外交部提出正式抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 purport</td><td>/pərˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>声称，打算</td><td>purport to be an expert<br>自称是专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several websites purport to offer free medical advice from real doctors.  有几个网站声称提供真医生的免费医疗建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 warrant</td><td>/ˈwɔːrənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保证，担保；使有正当理由</td><td>warrant the quality<br>保证质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory warrants the quality of every tool it sells abroad.  这家工厂为其出口的每件工具的质量提供保证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 capitulate</td><td>/kəˈpɪtʃuleɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>投降，默许</td><td>capitulate to their demands<br>屈从他们的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After weeks of protest the company capitulated to the workers' demands.  抗议数周后，公司向工人的要求屈服了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 quit</td><td>/ˈkwɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>退出，辞去；停止</td><td>quit the organization<br>退出该组织</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She quit the committee after the second disagreement.  第二次分歧之后她退出了委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 succumb</td><td>/səˈkʌm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>屈服；死亡</td><td>succumb to pressure<br>屈服于压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor finally succumbed to pressure and withdrew the unpopular proposal.  市长最终屈服于压力，撤回了不受欢迎的提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surrender</td><td>/səˈrendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>交出，放弃，投降</td><td>surrender to the police<br>向警方投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief finally surrendered to the police after a long chase.  经过长时间追捕，小偷终于向警方投降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 give in</td><td>/ɡɪv ˈɪn/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>屈服，让步</td><td>give in to pressure<br>在压力下让步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After hours of argument he finally gave in to her.  争论几小时后他终于向她让步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deny</td><td>/dɪˈnaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；拒绝给予</td><td>flatly deny the charge<br>断然否认指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not deny that the plan carries some very real risks.  我不否认这个计划确实带有一些实实在在的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gainsay</td><td>/ˌɡeɪnˈseɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认</td><td>gainsay the evidence<br>否认证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one could gainsay the evidence the inspector had collected.  没有人能否认检查员收集到的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contradict</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈdɪkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，反驳；与…相矛盾</td><td>contradict the evidence<br>与证据相矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness's second statement flatly contradicted everything she had said earlier.  证人的第二份陈述与她此前所说完全矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deny</td><td>/dɪˈnaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；拒绝给予</td><td>flatly deny the charge<br>断然否认指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not deny that the plan carries some very real risks.  我不否认这个计划确实带有一些实实在在的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disaffirm</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈfɜrm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，否定；废除</td><td>disaffirm the contract<br>否认合同效力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court may disaffirm a contract signed by a minor.  法院可以否定未成年人签署的合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disavow</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈvaʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>拒绝承认，否认</td><td>disavow any knowledge<br>否认知情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator moved quickly to disavow any knowledge of the secret payments.  参议员迅速表态，否认知晓那些秘密付款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disclaim</td><td>/dɪsˈkleɪm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝承认</td><td>disclaim responsibility<br>否认责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company quickly disclaimed any link to the failed venture.  公司迅速否认与那个失败项目有任何关联。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disconfirm</del></td><td>/ˌdɪskənˈfɜrm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证明为假，否证</td><td>disconfirm the hypothesis<br>否证该假设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New data disconfirmed the theory proposed a decade earlier.  新数据否证了十年前提出的理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disown</td><td>/dɪˈsoʊn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝承认</td><td>disown his own words<br>否认自己说过的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party quickly disowned the candidate's offensive remarks.  该党迅速否认与该候选人冒犯性言论有关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gainsay</td><td>/ˌɡeɪnˈseɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认</td><td>gainsay the evidence<br>否认证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one could gainsay the evidence the inspector had collected.  没有人能否认检查员收集到的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negate</td><td>/nɪˈɡeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；否定，取消</td><td>negate the effect<br>抵消效果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless error can negate weeks of careful work.  一个粗心的错误能抹掉数周的细致工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negative</td><td>/ˈneɡətɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>否决，反驳；负面的</td><td>negative the proposal<br>否决这项提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee negatived the motion after a short debate.  委员会经短暂辩论后否决了该动议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refute</td><td>/rɪˈfjuːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驳倒，反驳</td><td>refute the claim<br>驳斥这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scientist used new data to refute the claim about rising temperatures.  这位科学家用新数据反驳了关于气温上升的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reject</td><td>/rɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，驳回；废品</td><td>reject the proposal<br>否决提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee voted to reject the proposal without further discussion.  委员会投票否决了该提案，不再讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repudiate</td><td>/rɪˈpjuːdieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝接受</td><td>repudiate a claim<br>否认一项主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author repudiated the views quoted in the article.  作者否认了文章中引用的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acknowledge</td><td>/ækˈnɑːlɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>承认；对…表示感谢</td><td>acknowledge the mistake<br>承认错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor finally acknowledged that the report contained serious errors.  市长最终承认那份报告有严重错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 admit</td><td>/ədˈmɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>承认；准许进入</td><td>admit the mistake<br>承认错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally admitted that she had made a serious mistake.  她终于承认自己犯了一个严重的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 avow</td><td>/əˈvaʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开承认，坦率声明</td><td>avow one's beliefs openly<br>公开表明信念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He avowed his support for the strike in front of reporters.  他在记者面前公开表明支持这次罢工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concede</td><td>/kənˈsiːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>承认</td><td>concede the point<br>承认这一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally conceded that her opponent had made a fair point.  她最终承认对手说得有道理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confirm</td><td>/kənˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认，进一步确定</td><td>confirm the findings<br>证实这些发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Later tests confirmed what the first team had suspected.  后来的检测证实了第一个团队的猜测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contrived</b></td><td>/kənˈtraɪvd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不自然的，刻意的</td><td>a contrived happy ending<br>刻意安排的美好结局</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film's ending felt contrived and left the audience oddly unsatisfied.  这部电影的结局显得刻意，让观众莫名地不满足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affected</td><td>/əˈfektɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>装出来的，做作的</td><td>an affected accent<br>装出来的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke with an affected accent that fooled nobody present.  她用装出来的口音说话，没骗过在场任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assumed</td><td>/əˈsuːmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假装的，假冒的</td><td>under an assumed name<br>用假名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He traveled under an assumed name for several years.  他用假名旅行了好几年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bogus</td><td>/ˈboʊɡəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>伪造的，假冒的</td><td>a bogus claim<br>虚假的索赔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police discovered a stack of bogus passports in the van.  警方在货车里发现一叠伪造护照。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 factitious</td><td>/fækˈtɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>人为的，虚假的</td><td>factitious enthusiasm<br>装出来的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The applause sounded factitious and died away very quickly.  掌声听起来很假，很快就停了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feigned</td><td>/ˈfeɪnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假装的，伪装的</td><td>feigned surprise<br>假装的惊讶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He listened with feigned interest while checking his phone.  他一边看手机，一边假装感兴趣地听着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plastic</td><td>/ˈplæstɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可塑的；易受影响的</td><td>a plastic personality<br>易受塑造的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children have remarkably plastic minds and absorb new habits quickly.  幼儿的心智极为可塑，能很快吸收新习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pretended</td><td>/priˈtendɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假装的，虚伪的</td><td>pretended concern<br>假装的关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her pretended surprise did not convince anyone in the room.  她假装的惊讶没能说服屋里任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pseudo</td><td>/ˈsuːdoʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假的，冒充的</td><td>pseudo science<br>伪科学</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The book dresses up old superstitions as pseudo science.  这本书把旧迷信包装成伪科学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 simulated</td><td>/ˈsɪmjəˌleɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>模拟的，假装的</td><td>simulated leather<br>人造皮革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pilots train for storms inside a simulated cockpit.  飞行员在模拟驾驶舱里练习应对风暴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unnatural</td><td>/ʌnˈnætʃərəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不自然的，做作的</td><td>an unnatural smile<br>不自然的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice sounded stiff and unnatural on the recording.  录音里她的声音听起来僵硬而不自然。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 artless</td><td>/ˈɑrtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏技巧的，笨拙的</td><td>an artless imitation<br>拙劣的模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting was an artless copy of a famous portrait.  这幅画是对一幅名作的拙劣临摹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 natural</td><td>/ˈnætʃərəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自然的，正常的</td><td>a natural reaction<br>自然的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is natural to feel nervous before a big exam.  大考前紧张是很自然的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spontaneous</td><td>/spɑːnˈteɪniəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自发的，不经思索的</td><td>a spontaneous burst of applause<br>自发的一阵掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A spontaneous burst of applause followed the young pianist's final note.  年轻钢琴家弹出最后一个音，掌声自发响起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unaffected</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈfektɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不受影响的</td><td>unaffected by the crisis<br>未受危机影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Northern farms remained largely unaffected by the drought.  北方的农场基本未受这场旱灾影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 uncontrived</del></td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈtraɪvd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自然的，不做作的</td><td>uncontrived charm<br>不做作的魅力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film's power comes from its plain, uncontrived dialogue.  这部电影的力量来自朴素而不做作的对白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfeigned</td><td>/ʌnˈfeɪnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真诚的，不做作的</td><td>unfeigned delight<br>真心的喜悦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unfeigned joy at the news warmed the whole room.  她听到消息时真心的喜悦温暖了整个屋子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unforced</td><td>/ʌnˈfɔːrst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自然的，不勉强的</td><td>unforced laughter<br>自然的笑声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their easy, unforced rapport made the interview a pleasure.  他们自然轻松的默契让访谈非常愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>convene</b></td><td>/kənˈviːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>召开，召集</td><td>convene a meeting<br>召开会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board convened an emergency session to discuss the sudden resignation.  董事会召开紧急会议讨论这次突然辞职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assemble</td><td>/əˈsembəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>聚集，集合；组装</td><td>assemble a crowd<br>聚集起人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers assembled in the square to hear the announcement.  村民聚集在广场上听那则公告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 call</td><td>/ˈkɔːl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>召集，召开</td><td>call a special meeting<br>召开特别会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chairman called an emergency meeting for Monday morning.  主席召集了周一上午的紧急会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muster</td><td>/ˈmʌstər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>集合，召集；一群人</td><td>muster the troops<br>召集部队</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain mustered his crew on the upper deck.  船长把船员集合到上层甲板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 summon</td><td>/ˈsʌmən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>召集，召唤</td><td>summon the committee<br>召集委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general summoned all his officers before launching the attack.  将军在发动进攻前召集了所有的军官。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disband</td><td>/dɪsˈbænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>解散，遣散</td><td>disband the committee<br>解散委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The band disbanded after their final concert last year.  乐队在去年最后一场演唱会后解散。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dispel</td><td>/dɪˈspel/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驱散，消除（疑虑）</td><td>dispel the rumors<br>消除谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Clear figures from the auditor dispelled most of the doubts.  审计师提供的清晰数据消除了大部分疑虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disperse</td><td>/dɪˈspɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>分散，散开，驱散</td><td>disperse the crowd<br>驱散人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police asked the protesters to disperse before nightfall.  警方要求抗议者在天黑前散开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissipate</td><td>/ˈdɪsɪpeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>驱散，消散；浪费</td><td>dissipate the fog<br>驱散雾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The morning mist dissipated as soon as the sun rose.  太阳一升起，晨雾就消散了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissolve</td><td>/dɪˈzɑːlv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>溶解，融化；解散</td><td>dissolve in water<br>在水中溶解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stir the powder until the crystals dissolve in water completely.  搅拌粉末，直到晶体在水中完全溶解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 squander</td><td>/ˈskwɑːndər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浪费</td><td>squander an opportunity<br>浪费机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not squander this opportunity to study abroad for a year.  不要浪费这次出国留学一年的机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>correlate</b></td><td>/ˈkɔːrəleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使…相关联</td><td>correlate income with health<br>把收入与健康关联</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers correlated hours of sleep with performance on the memory tests.  研究者把睡眠时长与记忆测试的表现关联起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 connect</td><td>/kəˈnekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，联结</td><td>connect the two wires<br>连接两根导线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow tunnel connects the two halves of the building.  一条窄隧道把大楼的两半连起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 identify</td><td>/aɪˈdentəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>确认，辨认出</td><td>identify the main cause<br>确认主要原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers identified the main cause of the outbreak within days.  研究人员在几天内确认了疫情的主要原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relate</td><td>/rɪˈleɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>与…相关；叙述</td><td>relate to the topic<br>与主题相关</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His question did not relate to the topic under discussion.  他的问题与正在讨论的主题无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>counterpart</b></td><td>/ˈkaʊntərpɑːrt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（地位、功能）对等的人或物</td><td>his French counterpart<br>他的法国对等人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our finance minister met his German counterpart to discuss the trade dispute.  我们的财政部长会见德国的对等官员，商讨贸易争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coequal</td><td>/ˌkoʊˈiːkwəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>同等的，地位平等的</td><td>coequal branches of government<br>地位平等的政府分支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The constitution treats the three branches as coequal partners in governing.  宪法把三个分支视为地位平等的治理伙伴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 compeer</del></td><td>/kəmˈpɪr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>同辈，地位相当者</td><td>without a compeer<br>无人与之匹敌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a violinist she had no compeer in her generation.  作为小提琴家，她在同辈中无人匹敌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coordinate</td><td>/koʊˈɔːrdɪneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>协调，使配合</td><td>coordinate the relief effort<br>协调救援工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two agencies coordinated the rescue effort after the flood.  洪水过后两个机构协调了救援工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equivalent</td><td>/ɪˈkwɪvələnt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>等价的，相等的</td><td>the equivalent amount<br>等量的数额</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They offered her the equivalent amount in shares instead of cash.  他们提出用等量的股份代替现金付给她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fellow</td><td>/ˈfeloʊ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>同伴，同辈；家伙</td><td>his fellow researchers<br>他的研究同行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fellow researchers doubted the result at first.  他的研究同行起初怀疑这个结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 like</td><td>/ˈlaɪk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>相似的，像；喜欢</td><td>two of like mind<br>两个想法相似的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They were of like mind on almost every important question.  他们在几乎所有重要问题上想法相似。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 match</td><td>/ˈmætʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>与…相配，比得上</td><td>match his rival's skill<br>比得上对手的技艺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few players could match his skill on wet grass.  湿草地上少有球员比得上他的技艺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parallel</td><td>/ˈperəˌlel/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>相似的，对应的；平行的</td><td>a parallel case<br>类似的案例</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians found a parallel case in a much earlier war.  历史学家在一场更早的战争中找到了类似的例子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peer</td><td>/pɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>同等地位的人，同辈；好奇地凝视</td><td>respected by his peers<br>受同辈尊重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His work is widely respected by his peers in the field.  他的研究在业内同辈中广受尊重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rival</td><td>/ˈraɪvəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>对手，竞争者</td><td>a bitter rival<br>势不两立的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms have been rivals in this market for decades.  这两家公司在该市场竞争了数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>counterproductive</b></td><td>/ˌkaʊntərprəˈdʌktɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>起反作用的，反效果的</td><td>a counterproductive strategy<br>起反作用的策略</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Punishing every mistake proved counterproductive in the long run.  长远看，惩罚每个错误反而起了反作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feckless</td><td>/ˈfekləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无成果的，没有价值的；粗心不负责任的</td><td>a feckless young man<br>不成事的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His feckless handling of the accounts nearly ruined the shop.  他对账目粗心不负责的处理几乎毁了这家店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hamstrung</td><td>/ˈhæmstrʌŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>受制约的，无力施展的</td><td>hamstrung by regulations<br>受规章束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The agency was hamstrung by a lack of funding.  该机构因缺乏资金而无力施展。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ineffective</td><td>/ˌɪnɪˈfektɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无效的，不起作用的</td><td>an ineffective treatment<br>无效的治疗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old medicine proved completely ineffective against the new virus.  这种旧药对新病毒完全无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ineffectual</td><td>/ˌɪnəˈfektʃuəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无效的，徒劳的</td><td>an ineffectual protest<br>徒劳的抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ineffectual attempts to calm the crowd only made things worse.  他徒劳的安抚只让人群更加激动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inefficacious</del></td><td>/ˌɪnefɪˈkeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无效的，不能奏效的</td><td>an inefficacious remedy<br>不奏效的疗法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors abandoned the inefficacious drug after two failed trials.  两次试验失败后，医生放弃了这种无效药物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inefficient</td><td>/ˌɪnɪˈfɪʃənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>效率低的，浪费的</td><td>an inefficient old engine<br>效率低下的旧发动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heating system is old, noisy, and terribly inefficient.  这套供暖系统老旧、吵闹，而且效率极低。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inexpedient</del></td><td>/ˌɪnɪkˈspiːdiənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不明智的，不合时宜的</td><td>an inexpedient remark<br>不合时宜的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Raising the issue now would be inexpedient and might delay the vote.  现在提出这个问题并不明智，还可能拖延表决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 effective</td><td>/ɪˈfektɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有效的，起作用的</td><td>an effective solution<br>有效的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple hand washing is an effective way to prevent illness.  简单洗手是预防疾病的有效方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 effectual</td><td>/ɪˈfɛktʃuəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有效的，起作用的</td><td>an effectual remedy<br>有效的疗法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple hand washing proved the most effectual precaution available.  简单的洗手被证明是最有效的预防措施。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 efficacious</td><td>/ˌefɪˈkeɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有效的</td><td>an efficacious treatment<br>有效的治疗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old remedy proved surprisingly efficacious against mild seasonal coughs.  这个老方子治轻微的季节性咳嗽出乎意料地有效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 efficient</td><td>/ɪˈfɪʃənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>高效的，有效率的</td><td>an efficient filing system<br>高效的归档系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new software makes the whole process far more efficient.  新软件让整个流程效率高得多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expedient</td><td>/ɪkˈspiːdiənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有效的，方便的；权宜的</td><td>a politically expedient move<br>政治上权宜的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Delaying the vote was expedient but hardly honorable.  推迟投票是权宜之计，却谈不上光彩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 operant</td><td>/ˈɑːpərənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>起作用的，有效的</td><td>the operant factor here<br>此处起作用的因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cost, not quality, was the operant factor in their decision.  在他们的决定中起作用的是成本而非质量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>courteous</b></td><td>/ˈkɜːrtiəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>礼貌的，谦恭有礼的</td><td>a courteous reply<br>礼貌的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even when customers shouted, the young clerk remained calm and courteous.  即使顾客大声嚷嚷，那位年轻店员仍然平静而有礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 civil</td><td>/ˈsɪvəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>礼貌的；民事的</td><td>a civil reply<br>有礼的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to stay civil even when the discussion gets heated.  即使讨论变得激烈也要努力保持礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 civilized</td><td>/ˈsɪvəˌlaɪzd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>文明的，有教养的</td><td>civilized behavior<br>文明的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They settled the dispute in a calm, civilized manner over tea.  他们喝茶时以平静文明的方式解决了争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 couth</del></td><td>/kuːθ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，文雅的</td><td>a couth young man<br>一个有教养的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was surprisingly couth for someone raised on the streets.  对一个街头长大的人来说，他出奇地有教养。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 genteel</td><td>/dʒenˈtiːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，不粗俗的</td><td>genteel manners<br>有教养的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her genteel accent betrayed an expensive and very traditional upbringing.  她文雅的口音透露出昂贵而传统的成长背景。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gracious</td><td>/ˈɡreɪʃəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和善的，亲切的</td><td>a gracious host<br>亲切的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a gracious host who made every guest feel welcome.  她是位亲切的主人，让每位客人都感到受欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mannerly</td><td>/ˈmænərli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有礼貌的，举止得体的</td><td>a mannerly child<br>有礼貌的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mannerly boy thanked everyone before he left the table.  这个有礼貌的男孩离席前向每个人道谢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suave</td><td>/ˈswɑːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>老于世故的，圆滑的</td><td>a suave diplomat<br>圆滑的外交官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The suave diplomat deflected every awkward question gracefully.  这位圆滑的外交官优雅地挡开了每个尴尬问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 urbane</td><td>/ərˈbeɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>彬彬有礼的，文雅的</td><td>an urbane host<br>文雅的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our urbane host guided the conversation with easy wit and grace.  我们文雅的主人以轻松的机智和风度引导着谈话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-bred</td><td>/wɛl ˈbrɛd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，有礼貌的</td><td>a well-bred young woman<br>一位有教养的年轻女子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The well-bred guest thanked the host before leaving.  这位有教养的客人离开前向主人道了谢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discourteous</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrtiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，粗鲁的</td><td>a discourteous reply<br>无礼的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was discourteous to leave without thanking the host.  不向主人道谢就离开是无礼的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ill-bred</td><td>/ˌɪl ˈbred/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没教养的，粗野的</td><td>ill-bred behavior<br>没教养的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was ill-bred of him to leave without thanking the host.  他不谢主人就离开，实在没教养。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ill-mannered</td><td>/ˌɪl ˈmænərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>举止粗鲁的，没礼貌的</td><td>an ill-mannered guest<br>没礼貌的客人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ill-mannered boy grabbed food before the guests sat down.  这个没礼貌的男孩在客人坐下前就抓起食物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impertinent</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrtnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相关的；无礼的，鲁莽的</td><td>an impertinent remark<br>无礼的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer objected that the question was impertinent to the charges.  律师反对说，这个问题与指控无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impolite</td><td>/ˌɪmpəˈlaɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，粗鲁的</td><td>an impolite remark<br>无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is impolite to interrupt someone in the middle of a sentence.  在别人话没说完时插嘴很不礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impudent</td><td>/ˈɪmpjədənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>放肆大胆的，无礼的</td><td>an impudent remark<br>一句无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The impudent student argued with the professor in class.  这个无礼的学生在课上与教授争辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insolent</td><td>/ˈɪnsələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗野的，无礼的</td><td>an insolent tone<br>无礼的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The student's insolent reply to the teacher shocked the entire classroom.  这名学生对老师无礼的回答震惊了整个教室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rude</td><td>/ˈruːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁的，无礼的</td><td>a rude remark<br>无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was rude of him to leave without thanking the host.  他不谢主人就离开，很没礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>coy</b></td><td>/kɔɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>羞怯的；不愿明说的</td><td>coy about her past<br>对自己的过去讳莫如深</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was oddly coy about where she had spent the previous summer.  她对上一个夏天去了哪里出奇地讳莫如深。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 backward</td><td>/ˈbækwərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>向后的；落后的；羞怯的</td><td>a backward glance<br>回头一望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too backward to introduce himself to the newcomers.  他太腼腆，不敢向新来的人自我介绍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bashful</td><td>/ˈbæʃfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>羞怯的，害羞的</td><td>a bashful smile<br>一个羞涩的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bashful boy hid behind his mother whenever guests arrived.  每次有客人来，这个害羞的男孩就躲到母亲身后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diffident</td><td>/ˈdɪfɪdənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏自信的，羞怯的</td><td>a diffident newcomer<br>腼腆的新人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too diffident to argue with his famous professor.  他太拘谨，不敢与那位名教授争辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 introverted</td><td>/ˈɪntroʊˌvɜːrtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内向的，含蓄的</td><td>an introverted child<br>一个内向的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Being introverted, she preferred a quiet library to a noisy party.  由于性格内向，她宁愿待在安静的图书馆而不是喧闹的派对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 modest</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦虚的；不大的，适度的</td><td>modest about her success<br>对成功很谦虚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed modest about his award and thanked his team.  他对自己的奖项很谦虚，还感谢了团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recessive</td><td>/rɪˈsesɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐性的；内敛的</td><td>a recessive gene<br>隐性基因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His recessive nature kept him silent at every noisy family gathering.  他内敛的性格使他在每次热闹的家庭聚会上都沉默不语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retiring</td><td>/rɪˈtaɪrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>腼腆的，不爱交际的</td><td>a shy and retiring man<br>一个害羞内敛的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author was a retiring man who avoided interviews.  这位作家性格内敛，从不接受采访。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sheepish</td><td>/ˈʃiːpɪʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>腼腆的；难为情的</td><td>a sheepish grin<br>一个不好意思的笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a sheepish apology for forgetting our appointment again.  他为再次忘记约会难为情地道了歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shy</td><td>/ˈʃaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>害羞的，胆怯的</td><td>too shy to speak<br>害羞得不敢开口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too shy to introduce himself to the visitors.  他太害羞，不敢向来访者自我介绍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 withdrawn</td><td>/wɪθˈdrɔːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内向孤僻的，不与人来往的</td><td>become withdrawn<br>变得孤僻内向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy grew quiet and withdrawn after moving to the city.  搬到城里后，那男孩变得沉默孤僻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extroverted</td><td>/ˈekstrəˌvɜːrtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>外向的，性格开朗的</td><td>an extroverted student<br>外向的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her extroverted nature made her popular at every new school.  她外向的性格让她在每所新学校都很受欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immodest</td><td>/ɪˈmɑdɪst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不谦虚的，自负的；不检点的</td><td>an immodest boast<br>一句自负的吹牛话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seems immodest to list every award on the first page.  把每个奖项都列在第一页显得有些不谦虚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 outgoing</td><td>/ˈaʊtˌɡoʊɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>外向的，友好健谈的</td><td>an outgoing personality<br>外向的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His outgoing personality helped him make friends very quickly.  他外向的性格让他很快就交到朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>credit</b></td><td>/ˈkredɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>表扬，赞扬；信任</td><td>take credit for<br>把功劳归于自己</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She deserves credit for keeping the project alive.  她值得称赞，因为她让这个项目坚持下来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belief</td><td>/bɪˈliːf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>信念，相信</td><td>a firm belief<br>坚定的信念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her belief in the project never wavered, even after the setbacks.  即使遭遇挫折，她对项目的信念也从未动摇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 credence</td><td>/ˈkriːdns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>相信，信任</td><td>lend credence to<br>使…显得可信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Recent excavations lend credence to the old story of a buried city.  近期的挖掘使那个关于地下古城的传说变得可信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 faith</td><td>/ˈfeɪθ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>信任，信念；信仰</td><td>have faith in<br>信任，相信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They kept faith in their coach through a terrible season.  在糟糕的赛季里他们始终信任教练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trust</td><td>/trʌst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>信任，信赖</td><td>win someone's trust<br>赢得某人信任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It takes years to build trust and one lie to lose it.  建立信任要数年，一句谎言就足以失去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acclaim</td><td>/əˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>称赞</td><td>win critical acclaim<br>赢得评论界赞誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won wide critical acclaim across Europe and Asia.  她的第一部小说在欧洲和亚洲赢得了广泛的评论界赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accolade</td><td>/ˈækəleɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>赞扬，荣誉；奖励</td><td>win the highest accolade<br>赢得最高荣誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel received accolades from critics on both continents.  这部小说获得了两大洲评论家的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 applause</td><td>/əˈplɔːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>鼓掌；认可，赞许</td><td>a round of applause<br>一阵掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech was greeted with long and warm applause.  她的演讲赢得了长时间热烈的掌声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commendation</td><td>/ˌkɑːmənˈdeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>表扬，嘉奖</td><td>receive a commendation for bravery<br>因勇敢获得嘉奖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer received a commendation for rescuing the trapped driver.  这名警官因救出被困司机而获嘉奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
