<tr><td>↳反 dump</td><td>/dʌmp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>丢弃，倾倒；抛弃</td><td>dump the waste<br>倾倒废弃物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Factories once dumped chemical waste straight into the river.  工厂曾把化学废料直接倾倒进河里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forsake</td><td>/fərˈseɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底放弃，抛弃</td><td>forsake one's friends<br>抛弃自己的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would never forsake the people who raised him.  他绝不会抛弃养育他的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ethos</b></td><td>/ˈiːθɑːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>道德准则</td><td>the company ethos<br>公司的精神准则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teamwork lies at the heart of this small company's ethos.  团队合作是这家小公司精神准则的核心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ethics</td><td>/ˈeθɪks/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>道德规范</td><td>a code of ethics<br>道德准则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee revised its code of ethics after the scandal.  丑闻之后委员会修订了道德规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 morality</td><td>/məˈræləti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>道德；德行</td><td>public morality<br>公共道德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal raised hard questions about morality in politics.  这桩丑闻引出了政治道德的难题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 morals</td><td>/ˈmɔːrəlz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>道德，品行</td><td>loose morals<br>品行不端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel questions whether wartime morals differ from those of peacetime.  小说质问战时道德是否与和平时期不同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 norms</td><td>/ˈnɔːrmz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>规范，准则</td><td>social norms<br>社会规范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Newcomers quickly learn the unwritten norms of that tight-knit workshop.  新来者很快学会那间紧密作坊的不成文规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 principles</td><td>/ˈprɪnsəpəlz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>原则，准则</td><td>stick to his principles<br>坚守原则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She resigned rather than betray the principles she had taught for years.  她宁愿辞职也不背弃多年所教的原则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 standards</td><td>/ˈstændərdz/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>标准，准则</td><td>high ethical standards<br>高道德标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic holds all its staff to strict hygiene standards.  诊所要求全体员工遵守严格的卫生标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exorcise</b></td><td>/ˈeksɔːrsaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>除去</td><td>exorcise old fears<br>除去旧日恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Writing the letter helped her exorcise old fears about failure.  写那封信帮她除去了对失败的旧日恐惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cashier</td><td>/kæˈʃɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤职，开除；丢弃</td><td>cashier a corrupt officer<br>撤掉腐败军官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The army cashiered him after the inquiry closed.  调查结束后军方将他撤职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cast</td><td>/kæst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提出；抛弃</td><td>cast doubt on<br>对…提出质疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new evidence cast doubt on the official explanation.  新证据让官方的解释受到质疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ditch</td><td>/ˈdɪtʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>抛弃，摆脱；沟渠</td><td>ditch the old plan<br>放弃旧计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team ditched the old design after two failed tests.  两次测试失败后，团队放弃了旧设计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dump</td><td>/dʌmp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>丢弃，倾倒；抛弃</td><td>dump the waste<br>倾倒废弃物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Factories once dumped chemical waste straight into the river.  工厂曾把化学废料直接倾倒进河里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fling</td><td>/ˈflɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>扔，猛掷；一时放纵</td><td>fling the door open<br>猛地推开门</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She flung her coat onto the chair and sat down.  她把外套一甩扔到椅子上坐下了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jettison</td><td>/ˈdʒetɪsn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抛弃，放弃（想法、计划等）</td><td>jettison the proposal<br>放弃该提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company jettisoned the proposal as soon as funding became uncertain.  资金一变得不确定，公司就放弃了这项提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lose</td><td>/ˈluːz/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>失去；丢失</td><td>lose everything overnight<br>一夜间失去一切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless investors can lose everything overnight when a bubble finally bursts.  泡沫破裂时，粗心的投资者可能一夜之间失去一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pitch</td><td>/ˈpɪtʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（船、飞机）颠簸倾斜</td><td>pitch in heavy seas<br>在大浪中颠簸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boat pitched so badly that dishes slid off.  船颠簸得厉害，盘子都滑落了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reject</td><td>/rɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，驳回；废品</td><td>reject the proposal<br>否决提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee voted to reject the proposal without further discussion.  委员会投票否决了该提案，不再讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scrap</td><td>/skræp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抛弃</td><td>scrap the whole plan<br>废弃整个计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Managers decided to scrap the whole plan and start again from scratch.  管理层决定废弃整个计划，从头再来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shed</td><td>/ˈʃed/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>摆脱，去掉；脱落</td><td>shed excess weight<br>减去多余体重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company shed three divisions to survive the downturn.  公司剥离了三个部门以度过衰退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slough</td><td>/ˈslʌf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>摆脱，抛弃；脱皮</td><td>slough off dead skin<br>去掉死皮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The snake sloughs its skin several times a year.  这种蛇一年脱皮数次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 throw</td><td>/ˈθroʊ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>扔，投；（与 out 连用）逐出</td><td>throw out the intruder<br>把闯入者扔出去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guards threw the heckler out of the crowded hall.  保安把那个捣乱者扔出了拥挤的大厅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 toss</td><td>/ˈtɔːs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>扔，抛；抛弃</td><td>toss aside<br>随手丢在一边</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tossed the crumpled letter into the fire without reading it.  她没读就把那封皱信扔进了火里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unload</td><td>/ʌnˈloʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>卸货，卸下；倾诉</td><td>unload boxes from the van<br>从货车上卸下箱子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They unloaded all the furniture before the rain started.  他们在下雨前把家具都卸完了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adopt</td><td>/əˈdɑːpt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>采纳，采用；收养</td><td>adopt a new policy<br>采纳新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council voted to adopt stricter rules on noise at night.  市议会投票采纳了更严格的夜间噪音规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 employ</td><td>/emˈplɔɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>雇用；使用</td><td>employ new staff<br>雇用新员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory employs nearly six hundred workers in this town.  这家工厂在本镇雇用了近六百名工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 use</td><td>/ˈjuːz/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使用，利用</td><td>use the tool properly<br>正确使用工具</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We used the old barn as a workshop all summer.  整个夏天我们把旧谷仓当作工作间使用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 utilize</td><td>/ˈjuːtəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>利用，使用</td><td>utilize local resources<br>利用当地资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village utilizes wind power to pump water from the well.  这个村子利用风力从井里抽水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>explicate</b></td><td>/ˈeksplɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解释，说明</td><td>explicate a difficult text<br>解说一段难懂的文本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher patiently explicated the most difficult passage in the poem.  老师耐心地解说了这首诗中最难的一段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clarify</td><td>/ˈklerɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明，澄清</td><td>clarify the rules<br>澄清规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dean clarified the rules after weeks of confused complaints.  在数周混乱的投诉后，院长澄清了规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 construe</td><td>/kənˈstruː/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解释，理解为</td><td>construe the remark as praise<br>把那句话理解为赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not construe my silence as agreement with the plan.  别把我的沉默理解为赞同这个计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demonstrate</td><td>/ˈdemənstreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过证据）证明，表明</td><td>demonstrate the theory clearly<br>清楚地证明该理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The experiment demonstrated the theory clearly to every skeptical observer.  实验向每位持疑的观察者清楚证明了该理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demystify</td><td>/diˈmɪstəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使浅显易懂，阐明</td><td>demystify the process<br>阐明这一过程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The book demystifies statistics for readers who fear numbers.  这本书为怕数字的读者把统计学讲得浅显易懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elucidate</td><td>/iˈluːsɪdeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>阐明</td><td>elucidate the problem<br>阐明问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The footnotes elucidate the problem far better than the chapter itself does.  脚注比正文本身更好地阐明了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 explain</td><td>/ɪkˈspleɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解释，说明</td><td>explain the reason<br>解释原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you explain why the experiment failed the second time?  你能解释一下第二次实验为何失败吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expound</td><td>/ɪkˈspaʊnd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>详细阐述，解释</td><td>expound on his theory<br>详细阐述他的理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor expounded her views on urban design for an hour.  教授用一个小时详细阐述了她的城市设计观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illuminate</td><td>/ɪˈluːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明</td><td>illuminate the problem<br>阐明问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lecture illuminated a problem that had puzzled economists for years.  她的讲座阐明了一个困扰经济学家多年的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illustrate</td><td>/ˈɪləsˌtreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>说明，举例说明</td><td>illustrate the point<br>说明这一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These figures illustrate how quickly the population has grown.  这些数字说明人口增长得多么迅速。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interpret</td><td>/ɪnˈtɜːrprɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解释，阐明</td><td>interpret the results<br>解释结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good guide can interpret each painting in one minute.  好的导览员能在一分钟内解说每幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 simplify</td><td>/ˈsɪmpləˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>简化，使简明</td><td>simplify the application form<br>简化申请表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new software simplifies the way teachers record grades.  新软件简化了老师登记成绩的方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unriddle</del></td><td>/ʌnˈrɪdəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开谜团，阐明</td><td>unriddle an ancient text<br>解读古代文本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scholars spent decades trying to unriddle the inscription.  学者们花了数十年试图解开这段铭文之谜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obscure</td><td>/əbˈskjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含义模糊的；平凡的，不知名的</td><td>an obscure poet<br>不知名的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an obscure poet until a critic rediscovered his work decades later.  他一直是个不知名的诗人，直到几十年后一位批评家重新发现他的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exterminate</b></td><td>/ɪkˈstɜːrmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>根除，消灭</td><td>exterminate the entire colony<br>消灭整个群落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team worked for months to exterminate the invasive beetles.  团队花了几个月消灭那些入侵的甲虫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 annihilate</td><td>/əˈnaɪəˌleɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底摧毁，歼灭</td><td>annihilate the enemy<br>歼灭敌人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single flood could annihilate the entire harvest of the valley.  一场洪水就能彻底毁掉整个山谷的收成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decimate</td><td>/ˈdesəˌmeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>大量毁灭，大批杀死</td><td>decimate the population<br>使人口大量减少</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The epidemic decimated herds of cattle across three provinces.  这场疫病使三个省的牛群大量死亡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 efface</td><td>/ɪˈfeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>擦掉，抹去；使不显眼</td><td>efface all traces<br>抹去所有痕迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Time had effaced the inscription on the old gravestone.  时间已把这块老墓碑上的铭文磨去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eradicate</td><td>/ɪˈrædəˌkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>根除，消灭</td><td>eradicate rural poverty<br>根除农村贫困</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vaccination campaigns helped eradicate smallpox across the world.  接种运动帮助在全世界根除了天花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expunge</td><td>/ɪkˈspʌndʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>删除，消除</td><td>expunge a criminal record<br>消除犯罪记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court agreed to expunge the conviction from her official file.  法庭同意将该定罪从她的正式档案中删除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extirpate</td><td>/ˈekstərˌpeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>根除，灭绝</td><td>extirpate an invasive species<br>根除入侵物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers worked for years to extirpate the weed from their fields.  农民花了多年才把这种杂草从田里根除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liquidate</td><td>/ˈlɪkwɪˌdeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>肃清，消灭；清算</td><td>liquidate political opponents<br>肃清政治对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime tried to liquidate every opponent who remained in the capital.  该政权试图肃清留在首都的每一个反对者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obliterate</td><td>/əˈblɪtəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抹去；彻底消灭</td><td>obliterate all traces of it<br>抹去它的一切痕迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The March blizzard obliterated all traces of the early spring flowers.  三月的暴风雪抹去了早春花朵的一切痕迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raze</td><td>/reɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底摧毁，拆为平地</td><td>raze a village<br>将村庄彻底摧毁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The invading army razed the village and burned every field around it.  入侵军队将村庄彻底摧毁，并烧掉了周围所有田地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sweep</td><td>/ˈswiːp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扫除，清除</td><td>sweep away all opposition<br>扫除一切反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform swept away centuries of local privilege.  这项改革扫除了几百年的地方特权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wipe out</td><td>/ˈwaɪp ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>彻底消灭，抹去</td><td>wipe out the debt<br>抹去债务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One bad season wiped out the family's savings.  一个糟糕的季节抹光了这家人的储蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conserve</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>节约，保存，保护</td><td>conserve energy<br>节约能源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We must conserve water during the long summer drought.  漫长的夏季干旱期间我们必须节约用水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 preserve</td><td>/prəˈzɜːrv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保护，保存</td><td>preserve the old forest<br>保护这片老林</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trust works to preserve wetlands along the coast.  该基金会致力于保护沿海湿地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 protect</td><td>/prəˈtekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保护，防护</td><td>protect from harm<br>保护免受伤害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick gloves protect your hands from the cold.  厚手套保护你的双手免受寒冷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>extrapolate</b></td><td>/ɪkˈstræpəleɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过逻辑）推断</td><td>extrapolate from limited data<br>由有限数据推断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Analysts extrapolate future growth from limited historical economic data.  分析师根据有限的历史经济数据推断未来增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conclude</td><td>/kənˈkluːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结束；得出结论</td><td>conclude the ceremony<br>结束仪式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge concluded that the evidence was too weak.  法官得出结论认为证据太薄弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decide</td><td>/dɪˈsaɪd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>裁决，判定；决定</td><td>decide the case<br>对案件作出裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took the panel two days to decide who deserved the prize.  评审团花了两天才裁定谁该得奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deduce</td><td>/dɪˈduːs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>推断，演绎出</td><td>deduce the answer logically<br>合乎逻辑地推出答案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the footprints she deduced that two people had passed.  她从脚印推断出有两个人经过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 derive</td><td>/dɪˈraɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>推导，得出；源于</td><td>derive a formula<br>推导出一个公式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He derived the equation from three simple assumptions.  他由三个简单假设推导出这个方程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gather</td><td>/ˈɡæðər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>收集，聚集</td><td>gather information<br>收集信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers gathered signatures outside the library all weekend.  志愿者整个周末在图书馆外收集签名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 judge</td><td>/ˈdʒʌdʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>评判者，裁判；法官；判断</td><td>a panel of judges<br>一个评审团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three judges scored each performance without conferring with one another.  三名评委各自评分，互不商议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reason</td><td>/ˈriːzən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>理由，原因；推理</td><td>a good reason for<br>…的充分理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave no reason for leaving the meeting early.  他没有给出提前离开会议的理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 understand</td><td>/ˌʌndərˈstænd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>理解，懂得</td><td>understand the question<br>理解问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I finally understand why the experiment kept failing.  我终于理解实验为何一直失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conjecture</td><td>/kənˈdʒektʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>推测，猜测；（没有依据地）认为</td><td>pure conjecture<br>纯属推测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without records, any date for the painting is pure conjecture.  没有记录，这幅画的年代纯属推测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 guess</td><td>/ˈɡes/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>猜测，估计</td><td>make an educated guess<br>作出有根据的推测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody could guess how long the repairs would take.  没人猜得出修理要花多久。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 speculate</td><td>/ˈspekjuleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>推测，揣测</td><td>speculate about the cause<br>推测原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists can only speculate about the cause of the sudden extinction.  科学家只能推测这次突然灭绝的原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surmise</td><td>/sərˈmaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（根据不足的）推测，揣测</td><td>a mere surmise<br>只是一种揣测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without documents, his account is a mere surmise about what happened.  没有文件，他的说法只是对当时情形的揣测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fabulous</b></td><td>/ˈfæbjələs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>幻想中的，不真实的；极好的</td><td>fabulous creatures<br>神话中的生物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old maps often showed fabulous creatures swimming in distant seas.  古地图上常画着在远海游动的神话生物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chimerical</td><td>/kɪˈmerəkəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>空想的，荒诞不经的</td><td>a chimerical plan<br>空想的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their plan to colonize Mars by 2030 seems chimerical.  他们2030年殖民火星的计划显得荒诞不经。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dreamy</td><td>/ˈdriːmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>美妙的；恍惚的</td><td>a dreamy summer evening<br>美妙的夏夜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent a dreamy evening beside the quiet lake.  他们在静湖边度过了一个美妙的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illusory</td><td>/ɪˈluːsəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>幻觉的，虚幻的</td><td>an illusory advantage<br>虚幻的优势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The advantage proved illusory once the competitors lowered their own prices.  竞争者一降价，这一优势就被证明是虚幻的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 phantom</td><td>/ˈfæntəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>幻象，幻影；虚幻的</td><td>a phantom of the past<br>过往的幻影</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He kept chasing the phantom of an easy fortune.  他一直追逐轻松发财的幻影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unreal</td><td>/ənˈriːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚幻的，不真实的</td><td>an unreal silence<br>不真实的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empty city felt unreal in the early morning light.  清晨的光线里，空城显得不真实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 visionary</td><td>/ˈvɪʒəneri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的；不切实际的，空想的</td><td>a visionary founder<br>有远见的创始人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their visionary founder predicted the mobile market twenty years early.  他们有远见的创始人提前二十年预见了移动市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 awesome</td><td>/ˈɑːsəm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人惊叹的；极好的</td><td>an awesome achievement<br>惊人的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The view from the summit was awesome enough to silence the group.  峰顶的景色壮观得让全队都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 divine</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极好的，非凡的</td><td>a divine chocolate cake<br>美妙极了的巧克力蛋糕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The weather that week was simply divine.  那一周的天气简直美妙极了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fantastic</td><td>/fænˈtæstɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒诞的，异想天开的</td><td>a fantastic tale<br>荒诞的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fantastic story about talking wolves amused the children.  他关于会说话的狼的荒诞故事逗乐了孩子们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heavenly</td><td>/ˈhevənli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>天上的；美妙无比的</td><td>heavenly bodies<br>天体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Astronomers charted the heavenly bodies visible that clear night.  天文学家绘制了那个晴夜可见的天体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 marvelous</td><td>/ˈmɑːrvələs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极好的，了不起的</td><td>a marvelous performance<br>精彩的演出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They did a marvelous job restoring the old organ.  他们把这台老管风琴修复得非常出色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noble</td><td>/ˈnoʊbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>高贵的，贵族的</td><td>a noble bearing<br>高贵的气派</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The horse moved with a noble grace across the field.  那匹马以高贵的优雅姿态穿过田野。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prime</td><td>/ˈpraɪm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>首要的，最重要的</td><td>the prime concern<br>首要关切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting costs became the prime concern of the new management team.  削减成本成了新管理团队的首要关切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 splendid</td><td>/ˈsplendɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>壮丽的；极好的</td><td>a splendid marble hall<br>壮丽的大理石厅堂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the roof we had a splendid view of the harbor.  从屋顶望去，港口的景色极为壮丽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superb</td><td>/sʊˈpɜːrb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极好的，卓越的</td><td>a superb performance<br>一场卓越的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young violinist gave a superb performance that night.  那晚这位年轻小提琴手献上了卓越的表演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superior</td><td>/suːˈpɪriər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>更好的，占优势的；上级</td><td>superior to the rest<br>优于其余各方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This model is clearly superior to the older version.  这个型号明显优于旧版。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 terrific</td><td>/təˈrɪfɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极好的，了不起的；极大的</td><td>a terrific new job<br>一份很棒的新工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You did a terrific job on that presentation yesterday.  你昨天那个演讲做得非常棒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 actual</td><td>/ˈæktʃuəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，实际的</td><td>the actual cost<br>实际成本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actual cost was far higher than the original estimate.  实际成本远高于最初的估价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 existent</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪstənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>存在的，现存的</td><td>the existent records<br>现存的记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only two existent copies of that pamphlet remain in the archive.  档案馆里只剩那本小册子的两份现存副本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 existing</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>现有的，现存的</td><td>existing regulations<br>现有的规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We can upgrade the existing system instead of buying a new one.  我们可以升级现有系统，而不必新买一套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 real</td><td>/ˈriːl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，真正的</td><td>a real event<br>真实事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is based on a real event from 1943.  这部电影基于1943年的一件真实事件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 atrocious</td><td>/əˈtroʊʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极坏的，极其残忍的</td><td>an atrocious crime<br>残暴的罪行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court described the killing as an atrocious crime.  法庭称这起杀人事件是极其残忍的罪行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 awful</td><td>/ˈɑːfəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>糟糕的，可怕的</td><td>an awful smell<br>难闻的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An awful smell came from the forgotten lunchbox.  被遗忘的饭盒里飘出难闻的气味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 execrable</td><td>/ˈeksɪkrəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>糟糕的，恶劣的</td><td>execrable table manners<br>极糟糕的餐桌礼仪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hotel served execrable coffee at breakfast every morning.  酒店每天早餐提供的咖啡都糟糕透了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lousy</td><td>/ˈlaʊzi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多得很的，充满的；糟糕的</td><td>lousy with tourists<br>游客多得很</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In August the town is lousy with tourists from every corner of Europe.  八月这座小镇挤满了来自欧洲各地的游客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pathetic</td><td>/pəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>差劲的；可怜的</td><td>a pathetic excuse<br>差劲的借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their defense in the second half was frankly pathetic.  他们下半场的防守说实话很差劲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 poor</td><td>/pʊr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫瘠的，贫乏的；贫穷的</td><td>poor rocky soil<br>贫瘠的石质土壤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The harvest was poor after a whole summer without rain.  整个夏天没下雨，收成很差。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rotten</td><td>/ˈrɑːtən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>糟糕的；腐烂的</td><td>a rotten piece of luck<br>糟糕的运气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I felt rotten about missing her birthday dinner.  没赶上她的生日晚餐，我心里很难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 terrible</td><td>/ˈterəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可怕的；糟糕的</td><td>a terrible storm<br>可怕的风暴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They lost everything in a terrible fire last winter.  去年冬天一场可怕的火灾让他们失去一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wretched</td><td>/ˈretʃɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极差的；沮丧的</td><td>wretched living conditions<br>恶劣的居住条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Families lived in wretched conditions beside the flooded riverbank.  一些家庭在泛滥的河岸边过着极差的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fad</b></td><td>/fæd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>（短暂的）流行，时尚</td><td>a passing fad<br>一时的风尚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Standing desks turned out to be more than a passing fad.  站立式办公桌最终不只是一时的风尚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 buzz</td><td>/ˈbʌz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>嗡嗡叫；嘈杂声</td><td>buzz around the flowers<br>在花丛间嗡嗡飞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bees buzzed lazily above the clover.  蜜蜂在三叶草上懒懒地嗡嗡飞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 craze</td><td>/ˈkreɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一时的狂热，风尚</td><td>the latest fitness craze<br>最新的健身热潮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Skateboarding became a craze at our school that spring.  那年春天滑板在我们学校成了热潮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthusiasm</td><td>/ɪnˈθuːziæzəm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>热情，热忱</td><td>greet the plan with enthusiasm<br>热情地迎接该计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her enthusiasm for the project convinced the whole committee.  她对这个项目的热情说服了整个委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fashion</td><td>/ˈfæʃən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>时尚，流行；方式</td><td>go out of fashion<br>不再流行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wide silk ties went out of fashion decades ago.  宽丝绸领带几十年前就不流行了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trend</td><td>/ˈtrend/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>趋势，时尚</td><td>a growing fashion trend<br>日渐流行的时尚趋势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trend toward remote work continued after the crisis.  危机之后远程办公的趋势仍在延续。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vogue</td><td>/ˈvoʊɡ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>流行，时尚</td><td>in vogue<br>正流行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loose linen shirts are in vogue again this summer.  宽松亚麻衬衫今年夏天又流行起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 classic</td><td>/ˈklæsɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>典型的，经典的</td><td>a classic case of denial<br>典型的否认案例</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These symptoms are classic signs of severe dehydration.  这些症状是严重脱水的典型表现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fallible</b></td><td>/ˈfæləbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可能出错的</td><td>a fallible memory<br>可能出错的记忆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even the best experts are fallible, so check their figures.  即便最优秀的专家也可能出错，所以要核对他们的数字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 errant</td><td>/ˈerənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>误入歧途的，犯错误的</td><td>an errant husband<br>出轨的丈夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The errant student was suspended after ignoring three separate warnings.  这名行为不端的学生在忽视三次警告后被停学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fail-safe</td><td>/ˈfeɪlseɪf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有故障保险的，万无一失的</td><td>a fail-safe system<br>有故障保险的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reactor has a fail-safe system that shuts it down.  反应堆有一套故障保险系统可将其关闭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foolproof</td><td>/ˈfuːlpruːf/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>十分简单以至于不会失败的</td><td>a foolproof plan<br>万无一失的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineers promised a foolproof plan, but the machine jammed anyway.  工程师承诺了万无一失的方案，机器却还是卡住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infallible</td><td>/ɪnˈfæləbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>绝无错误的；万无一失的</td><td>an infallible method<br>万无一失的方法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No editor is infallible, however careful he may be.  再怎么细心，编辑也不可能绝无错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfailing</td><td>/ʌnˈfeɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无穷的，可靠的</td><td>unfailing patience<br>无穷的耐心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She showed unfailing patience with the youngest students.  她对最小的学生表现出无穷的耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fanciful</b></td><td>/ˈfænsɪfl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不切实际的，荒谬的</td><td>a fanciful explanation<br>荒谬的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her fanciful account of the accident convinced no one at all.  她对事故荒谬不切实际的描述完全没能说服任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 absurd</td><td>/əbˈsɜːrd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合理的，荒谬的</td><td>an absurd suggestion<br>荒谬的建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It's absurd to expect results in a single week.  期望一周内出结果是荒谬的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bizarre</td><td>/bəˈzɑːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>怪异的，离奇的</td><td>a bizarre coincidence<br>离奇的巧合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By some bizarre coincidence we wore the same coat.  出于某种离奇的巧合，我们穿了同款外套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crazy</td><td>/ˈkreɪzi/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疯狂的，荒唐的</td><td>a crazy idea<br>疯狂的主意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It sounded like a crazy idea until it actually worked.  这主意听着很疯，直到它真的成功了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foolish</td><td>/ˈfuːlɪʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愚蠢的，傻的</td><td>a foolish mistake<br>愚蠢的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was foolish of him to ignore the warning signs.  他忽视警示标志实在愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insane</td><td>/ɪnˈseɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒唐的，疯狂的</td><td>an insane plan<br>疯狂的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Driving in this thick fog would be insane.  在这种大雾里开车太疯狂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonsensical</td><td>/nɑːnˈsensɪkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒谬的，无意义的</td><td>a nonsensical argument<br>荒谬的论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions were nonsensical and helped nobody.  说明荒谬无理，对谁都没帮助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preposterous</td><td>/prɪˈpɑːstərəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒谬的，极其反常的</td><td>a preposterous claim<br>荒唐的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The idea that he acted alone is preposterous.  说他独自行动的说法荒谬至极。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unreal</td><td>/ənˈriːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚幻的，不真实的</td><td>an unreal silence<br>不真实的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empty city felt unreal in the early morning light.  清晨的光线里，空城显得不真实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wild</td><td>/ˈwaɪld/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒唐的，毫无根据的；野生的</td><td>a wild guess<br>瞎猜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That's a wild guess with no evidence behind it.  那是毫无根据的胡乱猜测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 realistic</td><td>/ˌriːəˈlɪstɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>现实的，切合实际的</td><td>a realistic goal<br>切合实际的目标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Let's set a realistic goal for this month.  我们为本月定一个切合实际的目标吧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reasonable</td><td>/ˈriːzənəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合理的，通情达理的</td><td>a reasonable request<br>合理的请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That seems like a perfectly reasonable request.  那似乎是完全合理的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>favoritism</b></td><td>/ˈfeɪvərɪtɪzəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>偏爱，偏袒</td><td>show blatant favoritism<br>明显偏袒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Promoting his own nephew looked like blatant favoritism to everyone.  提拔自己的外甥在所有人看来都是明显的偏袒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 favor</td><td>/ˈfeɪvər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赞同，好评；恩惠</td><td>win public favor<br>赢得公众好评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal quickly won favor among the younger members.  这项提议很快赢得年轻成员的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 one-sidedness</td><td>/ˌwʌnˈsaɪdɪdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>片面性，偏向一方</td><td>the one-sidedness of coverage<br>报道的片面性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers complained about the one-sidedness of the newspaper's election coverage.  读者抱怨报纸选举报道的片面性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 partiality</td><td>/ˌpɑːrʃiˈæləti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>偏爱；偏袒</td><td>a partiality for sweets<br>对甜食的偏爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was accused of partiality toward his own students.  教练被指责偏袒自己的学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prejudice</td><td>/ˈpredʒədɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>偏见，成见</td><td>racial prejudice<br>种族偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of contact slowly wore away his prejudice against the newcomers.  多年接触慢慢消磨了他对新来者的偏见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 tendentiousness</del></td><td>/tɛnˈdɛnʃəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>偏向性，有偏见</td><td>the tendentiousness of the report<br>该报告的偏向性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics attacked the tendentiousness of his historical account.  批评者攻击他历史叙述中的偏向性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impartiality</td><td>/ˌɪmpɑːrʃiˈæləti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>公正，不偏袒</td><td>judge with impartiality<br>公正地裁断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A juror must maintain impartiality despite pressure from public opinion.  陪审员必须在舆论压力下保持公正。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 neutrality</td><td>/nuːˈtræləti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>中立，不偏不倚</td><td>maintain strict neutrality<br>保持严格中立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small country preserved its neutrality throughout both world wars.  这个小国在两次世界大战中都保持中立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 objectivity</td><td>/ˌɑːbdʒekˈtɪvɪti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>客观性，公正</td><td>maintain journalistic objectivity<br>保持新闻客观性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reviewers are expected to judge submissions with strict objectivity.  评审人应以严格的客观性评判投稿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 open-mindedness</td><td>/ˌoʊpən ˈmaɪndɪdnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>开明，思想开放</td><td>praised for his open-mindedness<br>因开明而受赞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her open-mindedness made her an unusually good listener.  她的开明使她成为格外好的倾听者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unbiasedness</del></td><td>/ʌnˈbaɪəstnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无偏见，公正无私</td><td>the unbiasedness of the estimate<br>该估计的无偏性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Statisticians value the unbiasedness of this simple estimator.  统计学家看重这个简单估计量的无偏性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>featureless</b></td><td>/ˈfiːtʃərləs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏特征的</td><td>a featureless plain<br>毫无特征的平原</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The road crossed a flat, featureless plain for eighty miles.  这条路穿过八十英里平坦、毫无特征的平原。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beige</td><td>/beɪʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏特征的</td><td>a beige personality<br>毫无特色的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report describes him as a beige figure with no strong views.  报告把他描述成一个没有鲜明主张、缺乏特征的人物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 characterless</td><td>/ˈkɛrəktərləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无特色的；缺乏骨气的</td><td>a characterless building<br>毫无特色的建筑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moved into a clean but characterless apartment block.  他们搬进一栋干净却毫无特色的公寓楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 faceless</td><td>/ˈfeɪsləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无个性的，无名无姓的</td><td>faceless bureaucrats<br>毫无个性的官僚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Decisions were made by faceless bureaucrats far away.  决定由远处毫无个性的官僚做出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 indistinctive</del></td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈstɪŋktɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无特色的，难以分辨的</td><td>an indistinctive style<br>毫无特色的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her indistinctive style made the paintings hard to date.  她毫无特色的风格使这些画难以断代。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neutral</td><td>/ˈnuːtrəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>中立的；无刺激性的</td><td>remain neutral in disputes<br>在争端中保持中立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Switzerland stayed neutral throughout most of the twentieth century.  瑞士在二十世纪大部分时间保持中立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noncommittal</td><td>/ˌnɑːnkəˈmɪtəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不表态的，含糊的</td><td>a noncommittal answer<br>不表态的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister gave a noncommittal answer to every question.  部长对每个问题都给出不表态的回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vanilla</td><td>/vəˈnɪlə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>平淡无奇的；香草</td><td>plain vanilla software<br>最普通的软件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They chose a plain vanilla version without extra features.  他们选了没有额外功能的最普通版本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diagnostic</td><td>/ˌdaɪəɡˈnɑːstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>特征性的；诊断的</td><td>a diagnostic feature<br>特征性的特点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The narrow leaves are a diagnostic feature of this species.  狭长的叶子是该物种的特征性特点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discriminating</td><td>/dɪsˈkrɪməˌneɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有差别对待的；有鉴别力的</td><td>policies discriminating against women<br>歧视女性的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court struck down policies discriminating against women in hiring.  法院废除了在招聘中歧视女性的政策。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 distinct</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>清晰的，明显的</td><td>a distinct impression<br>清晰的印象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a distinct difference between the two dialects.  这两种方言之间有明显差别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 distinctive</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋktɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>特征性的，典型的</td><td>a distinctive style<br>独特的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her distinctive style makes each painting recognizable from across the gallery.  她独特的风格使每幅画在展厅另一端就能被认出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 distinguishing</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有区别性的，特征性的</td><td>a distinguishing mark<br>识别标记</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bird's red crest is its main distinguishing mark.  这种鸟的红冠是它主要的识别标记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 identifying</td><td>/aɪˈdentəˌfaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>用于辨认的，标识性的</td><td>identifying details<br>身份识别信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report removed all identifying details of the patients.  报告删去了病人所有身份识别信息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 peculiar</td><td>/pəˈkjuːliər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>奇怪的；特有的</td><td>a peculiar smell<br>一股怪味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a peculiar smell coming from the basement.  地下室传来一股奇怪的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 typical</td><td>/ˈtɪpəkəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>典型的，一贯的</td><td>a typical example<br>典型的例子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This building is a typical example of the local style.  这栋建筑是当地风格的典型代表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>feisty</b></td><td>/ˈfaɪsti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的，好争论的</td><td>a feisty debater<br>好争辩的辩手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The feisty debater challenged every point her opponent made.  这位好斗的辩手对对手的每个论点都提出挑战。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggressive</td><td>/əˈɡresɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的；积极进取的，强有力的</td><td>an aggressive marketing campaign<br>强势的营销活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog became aggressive whenever strangers approached the gate.  只要有陌生人靠近大门，这只狗就变得有攻击性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agonistic</td><td>/ˌæɡəˈnɪstɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好争斗的，对抗性的</td><td>agonistic behavior<br>对抗性行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The study measured agonistic behavior among captive wolves.  该研究测量了圈养狼之间的对抗性行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assaultive</td><td>/əˈsɔːltɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好攻击的，暴力的</td><td>assaultive behavior<br>暴力攻击行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient's assaultive behavior required constant supervision by trained staff.  这名病人的攻击性行为需要受过训练的人员持续看护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bellicose</td><td>/ˈbeləˌkoʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好战的，挑起争斗的</td><td>bellicose rhetoric<br>好战的言辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general's bellicose rhetoric alarmed the neighboring countries.  这位将军的好战言辞令邻国警觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belligerent</td><td>/bəˈlɪdʒərənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的，好战的</td><td>a belligerent tone<br>好斗的口气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered the question in a belligerent tone that shocked us.  他用一种令我们震惊的好斗口气回答了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 combative</td><td>/kəmˈbætɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的，好争辩的</td><td>a combative tone<br>好斗的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered every question in a combative tone.  他用好斗的语气回答每一个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contentious</td><td>/kənˈtenʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引起争论的；好争论的，好战的</td><td>a contentious issue<br>有争议的议题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Land ownership remains the most contentious issue in the peace negotiations.  土地所有权仍是和谈中最有争议的议题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disputatious</del></td><td>/ˌdɪspjuˈteɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好争辩的，爱吵架的</td><td>a disputatious committee<br>好争辩的委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The disputatious committee argued for hours without reaching any decision.  这个好争辩的委员会争了几小时也没有决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gladiatorial</td><td>/ˌɡlædiəˈtɔːriəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>角斗士的；激烈对抗的</td><td>a gladiatorial contest<br>角斗式的较量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate turned into a gladiatorial contest of egos.  辩论变成了一场自负者之间的角斗式较量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 militant</td><td>/ˈmɪlətənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>激进好战的；激进分子</td><td>a militant activist<br>激进的活动人士</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union took a militant stance in the pay talks.  工会在薪资谈判中采取了强硬立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quarrelsome</td><td>/ˈkwɔːrəlsəm/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好争吵的</td><td>a quarrelsome neighbor<br>爱争吵的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their quarrelsome neighbor complained about absolutely everything.  他们爱争吵的邻居对一切都抱怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 truculent</td><td>/ˈtrʌkjələnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好战的，好斗的；粗野的</td><td>a truculent response<br>咄咄逼人的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The truculent teenager argued with every teacher who tried to correct him.  这个好斗的少年跟每位试图纠正他的老师都吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pacific</td><td>/pəˈsɪfɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平和的，爱好和平的</td><td>a pacific temperament<br>平和的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He settled the quarrel with a few pacific words.  他用几句平和的话平息了争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 peaceful</td><td>/ˈpiːsfəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和平的，平静的</td><td>a peaceful settlement<br>和平的解决方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides finally chose a peaceful path out of conflict.  双方最终选择了走出冲突的和平道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 peace-loving</td><td>/ˈpis ˌlʌvɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>爱好和平的</td><td>a peace-loving nation<br>爱好和平的国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They describe themselves as a peace-loving nation with no territorial claims.  他们自称是没有领土要求的爱好和平的国家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>felony</b></td><td>/ˈfeləni/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>重罪</td><td>convicted of a felony<br>被判重罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was convicted of a felony and lost his license.  他被判重罪，并被吊销了执照。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crime</td><td>/ˈkraɪm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>犯罪，罪行</td><td>commit a serious crime<br>犯下重罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crime went unsolved for more than twenty years.  这起案件二十多年未破。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 misdemeanor</td><td>/ˌmɪsdəˈmiːnər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>轻罪，不端行为</td><td>charged with a misdemeanor<br>被控轻罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Shoplifting is treated as a misdemeanor in that state.  在那个州入店行窃被视为轻罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 peccadillo</del></td><td>/ˌpekəˈdɪloʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>小过失，小毛病</td><td>overlook his peccadilloes<br>忽略他的小过失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters forgave the mayor's peccadilloes because of his real achievements.  因为确有实绩，选民原谅了市长的小过失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ferocious</b></td><td>/fəˈroʊʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>激烈的，爆发性的；凶猛的，残暴的</td><td>a ferocious storm<br>猛烈的风暴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A ferocious storm tore the roof off the fishing shed.  一场猛烈的风暴把渔棚的屋顶掀掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cyclonic</td><td>/saɪˈklɑnɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>旋风般的，狂暴的</td><td>cyclonic winds offshore<br>近海的狂暴风势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The storm brought cyclonic gusts along the whole coast.  风暴给整条海岸带来了旋风般的阵风。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 explosive</td><td>/ɪkˈsploʊsɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>爆炸性的，暴烈的</td><td>an explosive temper<br>暴烈的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His explosive anger frightened everyone in the office.  他暴烈的怒气让办公室里所有人害怕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 furious</td><td>/ˈfjʊriəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狂怒的，暴怒的</td><td>furious with his son<br>对儿子大发雷霆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was furious when the airline lost her luggage.  航空公司丢了她的行李，她怒不可遏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 paroxysmal</td><td>/ˌpærəkˈsɪzməl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>突发性的，猛烈的</td><td>paroxysmal fits of coughing<br>突发性剧烈咳嗽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient suffered paroxysmal pain throughout the night.  病人整夜遭受突发性疼痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rabid</td><td>/ˈræbɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狂热的，不冷静的</td><td>a rabid supporter<br>狂热的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A rabid supporter of the team shouted insults at the referee.  一个狂热的球队支持者对裁判大声辱骂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stormy</td><td>/ˈstɔːrmi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>暴风雨的；激烈的</td><td>a stormy meeting<br>激烈的会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stormy meeting lasted until well after midnight.  这场激烈的会议一直开到午夜之后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tempestuous</td><td>/temˈpestʃuəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狂暴的，动荡不安的</td><td>a tempestuous relationship<br>剧烈动荡的关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their tempestuous marriage ended after only two stormy years.  他们剧烈动荡的婚姻仅两年就结束了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tumultuous</td><td>/tuˈmʌltʃuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喧闹的；动乱的</td><td>tumultuous applause<br>雷鸣般的掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pianist returned to tumultuous applause from the standing crowd.  钢琴家返场时全场起立，掌声雷动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 turbulent</td><td>/ˈtɜːrbjələnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>动荡的</td><td>a turbulent period<br>动荡的时期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They married during a turbulent period of war and economic collapse.  他们在战争与经济崩溃的动荡时期结了婚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 volcanic</td><td>/vɑlˈkænɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>火山般的，猛烈的</td><td>volcanic rage<br>火山般的怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His volcanic temper erupted over a trivial mistake.  他火山般的脾气因一个小错就爆发了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feral</td><td>/ˈferəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>野生的</td><td>a feral cat<br>野猫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A feral cat had been living under the empty porch.  一只野猫一直住在空荡的门廊下面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grim</td><td>/ˈɡrɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷酷的，不肯让步的；严峻的</td><td>grim determination<br>冷酷的决心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fought on with grim persistence until the very end.  他以冷酷的坚韧一直战斗到最后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 savage</td><td>/ˈsævədʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>凶残的，野蛮的；猛烈攻击</td><td>a savage beating<br>野蛮的殴打</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics savaged the film for its careless treatment of history.  批评家猛烈批评该片对历史的草率处理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vicious</td><td>/ˈvɪʃəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>凶恶的，恶毒的</td><td>a vicious circle<br>恶性循环</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog gave a vicious bite that required several stitches.  那只狗凶恶地咬了一口，需要缝几针。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nonviolent</td><td>/nɑnˈvaɪələnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非暴力的</td><td>nonviolent resistance<br>非暴力抵抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The movement stayed strictly nonviolent despite heavy provocation.  尽管遭到严重挑衅，运动始终坚持非暴力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 peaceable</td><td>/ˈpiːsəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>爱好和平的；平和的</td><td>a peaceable neighbor<br>平和的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaceable villagers settled disputes without ever calling police.  这些平和的村民解决纠纷从不报警。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 peaceful</td><td>/ˈpiːsfəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和平的，平静的</td><td>a peaceful settlement<br>和平的解决方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides finally chose a peaceful path out of conflict.  双方最终选择了走出冲突的和平道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gentle</td><td>/ˈdʒentəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和的，轻柔的</td><td>a gentle voice<br>温柔的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave the door a gentle push and stepped inside.  他轻轻推了一下门，走了进去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mild</td><td>/ˈmaɪld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和的，不严厉的</td><td>a mild punishment<br>轻微的处罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The winter here is unusually mild and rarely brings snow.  这里的冬天格外温和，很少下雪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unaggressive</del></td><td>/ˌʌnəˈɡrɛsɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不好斗的，温和的</td><td>an unaggressive breed of dog<br>不好斗的犬种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These large fish are surprisingly unaggressive toward divers.  这些大鱼对潜水者出人意料地温和。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fervent</b></td><td>/ˈfɜːrvənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满感情的，热情洋溢的</td><td>a fervent believer<br>热切的信徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a fervent believer in free public libraries for everyone.  她热切地相信应该为所有人提供免费的公共图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ardent</td><td>/ˈɑːrdənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，热切的</td><td>an ardent supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an ardent fan of the team for fifty years.  他五十年来一直是这支球队的狂热球迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demonstrative</td><td>/dɪˈmɑːnstrətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感情外露的</td><td>a demonstrative greeting<br>热情外露的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her demonstrative hugs surprised colleagues used to formal handshakes.  她热情的拥抱让习惯正式握手的同事很惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fervid</td><td>/ˈfɜːrvɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的</td><td>a fervid supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fervid speech moved even the quietest people in the room.  他热情洋溢的演讲连房间里最安静的人都被感动了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flaming</td><td>/ˈfleɪmɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>炽热的，火焰般的</td><td>flaming summer heat<br>炽热的夏日高温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The flaming afternoon sun made the empty market street unbearable.  炽热的午后阳光让空荡的集市街道令人难以忍受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glowing</td><td>/ˈɡloʊɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发光的，炽热的</td><td>glowing embers<br>发光的余烬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Glowing coals gave the little room a warm orange light.  发光的炭火给小屋带来温暖的橙色光线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impassioned</td><td>/ɪmˈpæʃənd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慷慨激昂的，充满激情的</td><td>an impassioned speech<br>慷慨激昂的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave an impassioned defense of public libraries.  她慷慨激昂地为公共图书馆辩护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incandescent</td><td>/ˌɪnkænˈdesənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>白热的，耀眼的</td><td>an incandescent bulb<br>白炽灯泡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incandescent filament burned out after only a few weeks.  白炽灯丝仅用几周就烧断了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 passionate</td><td>/ˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，充满激情的</td><td>a passionate defense of art<br>对艺术的热情辩护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is passionate about protecting the city's old neighborhoods.  她对保护城市的老街区充满热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 perfervid</del></td><td>/pərˈfɜrvɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极热情的，狂热的</td><td>perfervid enthusiasm<br>狂热的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His perfervid loyalty to the team bordered on obsession.  他对球队狂热的忠诚近乎痴迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torrid</td><td>/ˈtɔːrɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的，情感深厚的</td><td>the torrid zone<br>热带地区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The torrid afternoon heat kept everyone indoors until the sun finally set.  酷热的午后让所有人待在室内，直到太阳终于落下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vehement</td><td>/ˈviːəmənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（情感）强烈的，热情的</td><td>vehement opposition<br>强烈的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a vehement protest against the new tax.  她对新税提出了强烈的抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 zealous</td><td>/ˈzeləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，充满热忱的</td><td>a zealous supporter<br>热忱的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Zealous volunteers knocked on every door in the small town.  热忱的志愿者敲遍了小镇的每一户人家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cold</td><td>/ˈkoʊld/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冷的；冷淡的</td><td>a cold morning<br>寒冷的早晨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The water was much too cold for anyone to swim.  水太冷了，没人能游泳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cool</td><td>/ˈkuːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>凉爽的；冷淡的</td><td>a cool reception<br>冷淡的接待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His proposal met a cool response from the committee.  他的提案得到委员会冷淡的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dispassionate</td><td>/dɪsˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静客观的，不带感情的</td><td>a dispassionate analysis<br>冷静客观的分析</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge gave a dispassionate summary of both arguments.  法官冷静客观地总结了双方的论点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 emotionless</td><td>/ɪˈmoʊʃənləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感情的，冷漠的</td><td>an emotionless voice<br>毫无感情的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He read the verdict in a flat, emotionless tone.  他用平板毫无感情的语调宣读了判决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impassive</td><td>/ɪmˈpæsɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无感情的</td><td>remain impassive under pressure<br>压力下仍无动于衷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His face stayed impassive as the verdict was read aloud.  宣读判决时，他的脸上始终毫无表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unemotional</td><td>/ˌʌnɪˈmoʊʃənəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不动感情的，冷静的</td><td>an unemotional account<br>不带感情的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unemotional summary of the disaster unsettled the listeners.  她不带感情的灾难总结让听众不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>figment</b></td><td>/ˈfɪɡmənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>虚构的事物，幻觉</td><td>a figment of imagination<br>凭空想象之物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The threatening letter was a figment of his own imagination.  那封威胁信是他自己凭空想象出来的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chimera</td><td>/kaɪˈmɪrə/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>虚构的事物，幻觉</td><td>a mere chimera<br>纯粹的幻想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lasting peace seemed a mere chimera to the exhausted soldiers.  对疲惫的士兵来说，持久和平仿佛只是幻想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conceit</td><td>/kənˈsiːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>幻想，奇特的想法；自负</td><td>a poetic conceit<br>诗中的奇思妙喻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel rests on the conceit that memories can be traded.  这部小说建立在记忆可以交易的奇想之上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 daydream</td><td>/ˈdeɪˌdriːm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>白日梦，幻想</td><td>lost in a daydream<br>陷入白日梦中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window lost in a daydream about sailing away.  他坐在窗边，沉浸在扬帆远行的白日梦里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delusion</td><td>/dɪˈluːʒn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>错觉，妄想</td><td>labor under a delusion<br>抱着错误的幻想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He clung to the delusion that the company would soon rehire him.  他固执地抱着公司很快会重新雇用他的妄想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dream</td><td>/ˈdriːm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>梦想，幻想；做梦</td><td>dream of fame<br>幻想成名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a child he dreamed of sailing around the world.  他小时候幻想过环球航行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fancy</td><td>/ˈfænsi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>喜爱，想要</td><td>fancy a cup of tea<br>想喝杯茶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do you fancy a walk along the river this evening?  今晚想不想去河边散散步？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fantasy</td><td>/ˈfæntəsi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>幻想</td><td>indulge in fantasy<br>沉溺于幻想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her plan to retire at thirty was pure fantasy.  她三十岁退休的打算纯属幻想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hallucination</td><td>/həˌluːsɪˈneɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>幻觉</td><td>suffer hallucinations<br>出现幻觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The high fever gave him vivid hallucinations that lasted through the night.  高烧让他产生了持续整夜的鲜明幻觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illusion</td><td>/ɪˈluːʒən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>错觉，幻想</td><td>an optical illusion<br>视觉错觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mirrors create the illusion that the room is twice as large.  镜子造成了房间大了一倍的错觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 phantasm</td><td>/ˈfæntæzəm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>幻象，幻影</td><td>a fleeting phantasm<br>转瞬即逝的幻象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In the fog the tower looked like a phantasm floating above water.  雾中那座塔像是漂浮在水面上的幻影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vision</td><td>/ˈvɪʒən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>幻象，梦幻；视力；远见</td><td>have a vision of<br>看到…的幻象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The saint claimed to have seen a vision of a burning city.  这位圣徒声称看到过一座燃烧城市的幻象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fact</td><td>/ˈfækt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>事实，实情</td><td>a matter of fact<br>事实问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Whether the letter existed is a question of fact, not opinion.  那封信是否存在是事实问题，不是意见问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 materiality</td><td>/məˌtɪriˈæləti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>实在性，物质性</td><td>the materiality of the evidence<br>证据的实在性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The philosopher questioned the materiality of everything we perceive.  这位哲学家质疑我们所感知一切的实在性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reality</td><td>/riˈæləti/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>现实，真实</td><td>face reality<br>面对现实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sooner or later he must face the reality of his debts.  他迟早得面对自己债务的现实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fitful</b></td><td>/ˈfɪtfl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>断断续续的</td><td>a fitful night's sleep<br>断断续续的一夜睡眠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Noise from the street gave her a fitful night's sleep.  街上的噪音让她一夜睡得断断续续。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aperiodic</td><td>/ˌeɪpɪriˈɑdɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非周期的，无规律的</td><td>aperiodic seismic tremors<br>无规律的地震颤动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The signal turned out to be aperiodic and hard to model.  这个信号最终被证明是非周期的，难以建模。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 casual</td><td>/ˈkæʒəwəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>随意的，非故意的</td><td>a casual remark<br>随口的一句话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A casual remark at dinner accidentally revealed the whole secret.  晚餐时随口一句话意外揭出了整个秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discontinuous</td><td>/ˌdɪskənˈtɪnjuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不连续的，间断的</td><td>a discontinuous record<br>不连续的记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fossil record here is discontinuous and full of long gaps.  这里的化石记录是间断的，有很多长期空缺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 episodic</td><td>/ˌepɪˈsɑːdɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>间歇性的；不定期的</td><td>episodic bursts of rain<br>断断续续的阵雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Episodic bursts of rain kept interrupting the outdoor ceremony all afternoon.  断断续续的阵雨整个下午不断打断露天典礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 erratic</td><td>/ɪˈrætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复无常的，不规律的</td><td>erratic driving behavior<br>飘忽不定的驾驶行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His erratic driving behavior alarmed the passengers long before the police stopped him.  早在警察拦下他之前，他飘忽不定的驾驶行为就让乘客惊慌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intermittent</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈmɪtənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>断断续续的</td><td>intermittent power failures<br>断断续续的停电</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Intermittent power failures made it impossible to finish the experiment that night.  断断续续的停电使当晚无法完成那项实验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irregular</td><td>/ɪˈreɡjələr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不规则的，不平整的</td><td>an irregular shape<br>不规则的形状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path was made of irregular stones of many sizes.  这条小路由各种大小不规则的石头铺成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 occasional</td><td>/əˈkeɪʒənəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偶尔的，间或的</td><td>occasional showers<br>偶有阵雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He makes occasional visits to his hometown in autumn.  他秋天偶尔回故乡看看。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sporadic</td><td>/spəˈrædɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>断断续续的</td><td>sporadic gunfire<br>断续的枪声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sporadic gunfire continued through the night on the edge of town.  镇子边缘整夜断断续续传来枪声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsteady</td><td>/ʌnˈstedi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不稳的，摇晃的</td><td>unsteady hands<br>不稳的手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice was unsteady as she read the letter to us.  她给我们读那封信时声音颤抖不稳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constant</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不变的，恒定的；忠诚的</td><td>a constant friend<br>忠诚不变的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her support remained constant through every difficult year.  她的支持在每个艰难年头都不曾改变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continuous</td><td>/kənˈtɪnjuːəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连续的，不间断的</td><td>a continuous line<br>一条连续的线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The machine has been in continuous operation for six days.  这台机器已连续运转六天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incessant</td><td>/ɪnˈsesnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无间断的</td><td>incessant noise<br>不间断的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incessant drumming from next door kept us awake all night.  隔壁不间断的鼓声让我们整夜无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fleeting</b></td><td>/ˈfliːtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；转瞬即逝的</td><td>a fleeting moment<br>短暂的瞬间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For one fleeting moment the clouds parted above the valley.  有一个短暂的瞬间，云在山谷上方散开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brief</td><td>/ˈbriːf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简短的，简洁的</td><td>a brief summary<br>简短的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a brief account of the accident to the police.  他向警方简短叙述了事故经过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ephemeral</td><td>/ɪˈfemərəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，转瞬即逝的</td><td>ephemeral fame<br>短暂的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internet fame is often ephemeral and quickly forgotten.  网络名声往往短暂，很快被遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evanescent</td><td>/ˌevəˈnesnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，逐渐消失的</td><td>evanescent beauty<br>转瞬即逝的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The evanescent glow of fireflies lit the summer path.  萤火虫短暂的微光照亮了夏夜小径。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flash</td><td>/ˈflæʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>闪光；闪耀，闪现</td><td>a flash of lightning<br>一道闪电</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A light flashed briefly in the window across the street.  街对面的窗子里闪了一下光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fugitive</td><td>/ˈfjuːdʒətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>短暂的，易逝的；逃亡的</td><td>a fugitive impression<br>转瞬即逝的印象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fugitive smile crossed her face before she resumed her serious expression.  一丝转瞬即逝的笑意掠过她的脸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impermanent</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrmənənt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非永久的，短暂的</td><td>impermanent beauty<br>短暂的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All fashions are impermanent, replaced by new styles within a few seasons.  一切时尚都是短暂的，几季之内就被新风格取代。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 passing</td><td>/ˈpæsɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，一时的</td><td>a passing fancy<br>一时的兴致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His interest in sailing was only a passing enthusiasm.  他对航海的兴趣只是一时的热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temporary</td><td>/ˈtempəreri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>暂时的，临时的</td><td>a temporary solution<br>临时的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company hired temporary staff to handle the busy holiday season.  公司雇了临时员工来应对繁忙的假日季。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transient</td><td>/ˈtrænʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；暂时的；过往的</td><td>transient population<br>流动人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fame in the internet age is often transient and quickly forgotten.  网络时代的名声往往短暂，很快被人遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transitory</td><td>/ˈtrænsətɔːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的</td><td>transitory pleasure<br>短暂的快乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His anger was transitory and he apologized within the hour.  他的愤怒很短暂，一小时内就道了歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ceaseless</td><td>/ˈsiːslɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不停的，持续的</td><td>ceaseless noise<br>不停的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceaseless traffic outside made it impossible for us to sleep.  外面不停的车流让我们无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deathless</td><td>/ˈdɛθləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不死的，不朽的</td><td>deathless fame<br>不朽的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet won deathless fame with a single slender volume.  这位诗人凭一本薄薄的诗集赢得了不朽名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eternal</td><td>/ɪˈtɜːrnl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>eternal life<br>永生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem promises the beloved eternal youth.  这首诗许诺爱人永恒的青春。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 everlasting</td><td>/ˌevərˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，持久的</td><td>everlasting peace<br>永久的和平</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They vowed everlasting loyalty to each other.  他们发誓永远彼此忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immortal</td><td>/ɪˈmɔːrtəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不朽的，永生的</td><td>immortal fame<br>不朽的声名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet's immortal lines are still quoted two thousand years later.  这位诗人不朽的诗句两千年后仍被引用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lasting</td><td>/ˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，永久的</td><td>a lasting impression<br>持久的印象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her kindness left a lasting impression on everyone in the ward.  她的善意给病房里每个人留下持久印象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 permanent</td><td>/ˈpɜːrmənənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>a permanent solution<br>永久的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They are seeking a permanent solution rather than another quick fix.  他们在寻找永久的解决办法，而不是又一个权宜之计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perpetual</td><td>/pərˈpetʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不断的</td><td>perpetual motion<br>永恒运动，永动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lives in perpetual fear of losing his job.  他一直生活在失业的恐惧中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 timeless</td><td>/ˈtaɪmləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不受时间影响的</td><td>timeless design<br>经典不过时的设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dress has a timeless elegance that never dates.  这条裙子有一种永不过时的优雅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>forage</b></td><td>/ˈfɔːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>寻找</td><td>forage for wild mushrooms<br>四处寻找野菇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children foraged for wild mushrooms along the damp forest path.  孩子们沿着潮湿的林间小路寻找野菇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chase</td><td>/tʃeɪs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>驱赶</td><td>chase away<br>赶走，驱散</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer chased the crows away from his young corn.  农夫把乌鸦从他的嫩玉米地里赶走。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hunt</td><td>/ˈhʌnt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>搜寻；打猎</td><td>hunt for clues<br>搜寻线索</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Divers hunted for wreckage along the seabed for three weeks.  潜水员在海床上搜寻残骸达三周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pursue</td><td>/pərˈsuː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>追求，追寻；追捕</td><td>pursue a career<br>追求事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pursued him for months before he finally agreed to dinner.  她追了他好几个月，他才终于答应共进晚餐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quest</td><td>/ˈkwest/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>寻找，探求</td><td>quest after the truth<br>探求真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hounds quested through the tall grass for hours.  猎犬在高草丛中搜寻了好几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rummage</td><td>/ˈrʌmɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>翻找，搜寻</td><td>rummage in the attic<br>在阁楼里翻找</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She rummaged in her bag for a pen.  她在包里翻找一支笔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 search</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>搜寻，搜查</td><td>search the whole area<br>搜遍整片区域</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers searched the woods until the light finally failed.  志愿者搜寻树林直到天色全暗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignore</td><td>/ˌɪɡˈnɔːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视，不理</td><td>ignore the warning<br>忽视警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain ignored the warning and sailed into the storm.  船长忽视了警告，驶入了风暴之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 neglect</td><td>/nəˈɡlekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>忽视，疏忽；忽略，怠慢</td><td>neglect his duties<br>疏忽职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He neglected his duties for weeks and was finally dismissed.  他数周疏忽职责，最终被解雇了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>forebode</b></del></td><td>/fɔːrˈboʊd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>预示不祥；有预感</td><td>forebode coming trouble<br>预示麻烦将至</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden silence in the forest seemed to forebode coming trouble.  林中突然的寂静似乎预示麻烦将至。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 augur</td><td>/ˈɔːɡər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>预言家，占卜官；预言</td><td>augur well for the future<br>预示前景良好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The Roman augur studied the flight of birds before every battle.  这位罗马占卜官在每场战役前都观察鸟的飞行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predict</td><td>/prɪˈdɪkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预言，预测</td><td>predict the outcome<br>预测结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one could predict how the jury would finally vote.  没人能预测陪审团最终会如何表决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 promise</td><td>/ˈprɑːməs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预示，有…的可能</td><td>promise a fine harvest<br>预示丰收</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Those heavy clouds promise rain before evening.  那些厚重的云预示天黑前会下雨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>foreground</b></td><td>/ˈfɔːrɡraʊnd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>强调，重视</td><td>foreground an issue<br>突出某一问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report foregrounds the needs of rural communities.  这份报告着重强调农村社区的需求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accent</td><td>/ˈæksent/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>强调，突出；口音</td><td>accent the positive<br>强调积极的一面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new design accents clean lines and natural light.  新设计突出了简洁的线条和自然光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accentuate</td><td>/əkˈsentʃueɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强调，突出</td><td>accentuate the difference<br>突出差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bright lighting accentuated every flaw in the painting.  明亮的灯光突显出这幅画的每处瑕疵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 emphasize</td><td>/ˈemfəˌsaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强调，着重</td><td>emphasize the importance of<br>强调……的重要性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She emphasized that the deadline could not be moved.  她强调截止日期不能改动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feature</td><td>/ˈfiːtʃər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>想象，设想；以…为特色</td><td>hard to feature it<br>难以想象那情形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I simply cannot feature him living quietly in the countryside.  我实在无法想象他在乡下安静生活的样子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 highlight</td><td>/ˈhaɪˌlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>强调，突出</td><td>highlight the main findings<br>强调主要发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The study highlights the risks of sitting all day.  这项研究强调了整天坐着的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illuminate</td><td>/ɪˈluːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明</td><td>illuminate the problem<br>阐明问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lecture illuminated a problem that had puzzled economists for years.  她的讲座阐明了一个困扰经济学家多年的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 press</td><td>/ˈpres/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>按，压；催促</td><td>press the button<br>按下按钮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He pressed the button twice before the lift moved.  他按了两次按钮电梯才动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 punctuate</td><td>/ˈpʌŋktʃueɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>突出强调；不时打断</td><td>punctuate the speech with jokes<br>演讲中不时插入笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud applause punctuated her acceptance speech throughout.  响亮的掌声不时打断她的获奖致辞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stress</td><td>/ˈstres/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>强调，着重</td><td>stress the importance<br>强调重要性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach stressed defense in every session this week.  本周每次训练教练都强调防守。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 de-emphasize</td><td>/diˈɛmfəsaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不再强调，减轻重视</td><td>de-emphasize test scores<br>淡化考试分数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new policy de-emphasizes test scores in admissions decisions.  新政策在录取决定中不再强调考试分数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 understate</td><td>/ˌʌndərˈsteɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻描淡写；低估</td><td>understate the risks<br>低估风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials understated the flood damage to avoid alarming the public.  官员们对洪灾损失轻描淡写，以免引起公众恐慌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 play down</td><td>/pleɪ ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>轻描淡写，淡化</td><td>play down the risks<br>淡化风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials tried to play down the scale of the leak.  官员们试图淡化这次泄漏的规模。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>forfeit</b></td><td>/ˈfɔːrfət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>丧失，被没收（权利、财物）</td><td>forfeit the right to appeal<br>丧失上诉权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He forfeited his right to appeal by missing the deadline.  他因错过期限而丧失了上诉权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 damages</td><td>/ˈdæmədʒəz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>损害赔偿金</td><td>sue for damages<br>起诉索赔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court awarded her substantial damages for the injury.  法院判给她一笔可观的伤害赔偿金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fine</td><td>/ˈfaɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>细微的，精细的</td><td>a fine distinction<br>细微的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drew a fine distinction between courage and recklessness.  他在勇敢与莽撞之间划出了细微的区别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forfeiture</td><td>/ˈfɔːrfətʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>丧失，没收（物）</td><td>forfeiture of the deposit<br>定金的丧失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Breaking the terms meant forfeiture of the entire bond.  违反条款意味着丧失全部保证金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 mulct</del></td><td>/ˈmʌlkt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>罚金；罚款，骗取</td><td>pay a heavy mulct<br>缴纳重罚金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The merchant was mulcted a hundred pounds for the offense.  这名商人因该罪行被罚一百英镑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
