<tr><td>↳近 penalty</td><td>/ˈpenəlti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>罚款，处罚</td><td>pay a stiff penalty<br>缴纳重罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Late filing carries a penalty of two hundred dollars.  逾期申报要罚两百美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bonus</td><td>/ˈboʊnəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>奖金，额外好处</td><td>an annual year-end bonus<br>年终奖金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every employee received a modest bonus in December.  每位员工十二月都拿到了一笔小额奖金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 premium</td><td>/ˈpriːmiəm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>高端的</td><td>premium quality goods<br>高端品质商品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shop sells only premium coffee beans from small farms.  这家店只卖来自小农场的高端咖啡豆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prize</td><td>/ˈpraɪz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>奖品，奖金</td><td>win first prize<br>赢得头奖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her short story won the magazine's annual prize.  她的短篇小说获得了该杂志的年度奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>forsake</b></td><td>/fərˈseɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底放弃，抛弃</td><td>forsake one's friends<br>抛弃自己的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would never forsake the people who raised him.  他绝不会抛弃养育他的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abandon</td><td>/əˈbændən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>放纵，尽情；放弃</td><td>with reckless abandon<br>肆意放纵地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She danced with wild abandon at the wedding reception.  她在婚宴上尽情忘我地跳舞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 desert</td><td>/dɪˈzɜːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抛弃，遗弃</td><td>desert one's post<br>擅离职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain refused to desert his crew in the storm.  船长拒绝在风暴中抛下船员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maroon</td><td>/məˈruːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>把…遗弃在孤岛上，使孤立无援</td><td>maroon on a desert island<br>遗弃在荒岛上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mutineers marooned the captain on a tiny uninhabited island.  叛变者把船长遗弃在一个荒无人烟的小岛上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quit</td><td>/ˈkwɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>退出，辞去；停止</td><td>quit the organization<br>退出该组织</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She quit the committee after the second disagreement.  第二次分歧之后她退出了委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renounce</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式放弃，声明弃绝</td><td>renounce one's claim<br>声明放弃权利主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He renounced his claim to the inheritance in writing.  他以书面形式放弃了继承权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strand</td><td>/strænd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遗弃，使置于困境</td><td>stranded on an island<br>被困在岛上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The storm stranded dozens of travelers on the small island overnight.  暴风雨使数十名旅客一夜困在小岛上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reclaim</td><td>/riˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>感化，使改造；开垦，回收</td><td>reclaim young offenders<br>感化少年犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity tries to reclaim addicts through work and counseling.  该慈善机构试图通过工作和咨询感化成瘾者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>frosty</b></td><td>/ˈfrɔːsti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冷的；无强烈感情的，冷淡的</td><td>a frosty welcome<br>冷淡的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The visitors received a frosty welcome from the village elders.  来访者受到村中长者冷淡的接待。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 algid</del></td><td>/ˈældʒɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冷的，冰冷的</td><td>an algid winter night<br>寒冷的冬夜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The algid wind off the lake numbed their faces within minutes.  湖面吹来的寒风几分钟就冻麻了他们的脸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arctic</td><td>/ˈɑːrktɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极寒的；北极的</td><td>arctic temperatures<br>极寒的气温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An arctic wind swept across the empty parking lot.  一阵极寒的风扫过空荡的停车场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bitter</td><td>/ˈbɪtər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严寒刺骨的；苦的</td><td>a bitter north wind<br>刺骨的北风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We waited an hour in bitter cold for the train.  我们在刺骨的严寒中等了一小时火车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chilling</td><td>/ˈtʃɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺骨的；令人恐惧的</td><td>a chilling wind<br>刺骨的寒风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A chilling drizzle soaked us before we reached shelter.  刺骨的细雨在我们到达庇护处前把我们淋透。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 coldish</del></td><td>/ˈkoʊldɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有点冷的，微寒的</td><td>a coldish spring morning<br>微寒的春晨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was coldish outside, so we took our coats.  外面有点冷，我们就带上了外套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cool</td><td>/ˈkuːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>凉爽的；冷淡的</td><td>a cool reception<br>冷淡的接待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His proposal met a cool response from the committee.  他的提案得到委员会冷淡的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 freezing</td><td>/ˈfriːzɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极冷的，冰冻的</td><td>freezing temperatures<br>冰点以下的气温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We waited on the freezing platform for half an hour.  我们在极冷的站台上等了半小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frigid</td><td>/ˈfrɪdʒɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严寒的；冷漠的</td><td>a frigid stare<br>冷冰冰的凝视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the latecomer a frigid stare and said nothing.  她冷冰冰地盯了迟到者一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 gelid</del></td><td>/ˈdʒɛlɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冰冷的，极寒的</td><td>gelid mountain water<br>冰冷的山泉水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He washed his face in the gelid stream and gasped.  他在冰冷的溪水里洗脸，冷得直吸气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glacial</td><td>/ˈɡleɪsiəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极冷的；冷漠的，无感情的</td><td>a glacial silence<br>冰冷的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His glacial tone made everyone at the table stop talking at once.  他冰冷的语气让桌边所有人立刻停止了交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 icy</td><td>/ˈaɪsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冰冷的；冷淡的</td><td>an icy road<br>结冰的道路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave him an icy stare and said nothing.  她冷冰冰地瞪了他一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nippy</td><td>/ˈnɪpi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺骨的，寒冷的</td><td>a nippy autumn wind<br>刺骨的秋风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It turned nippy as soon as the sun went down.  太阳一落山天就变得刺骨地冷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 numbing</td><td>/ˈnʌmɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>使麻木的，冻得发麻的</td><td>numbing cold on deck<br>甲板上冻得发麻的严寒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The numbing wind made it hard to hold the rope.  刺骨发麻的寒风让人难以抓住绳子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 polar</td><td>/ˈpoʊlər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全对立的；极地的</td><td>polar opposites<br>截然相反的两极</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brothers are polar opposites in almost every way.  兄弟俩几乎在各方面都截然相反。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snappy</td><td>/ˈsnæpi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>时髦利落的；简洁的</td><td>a snappy jacket<br>利落时髦的外套</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in a snappy grey suit and polished shoes.  他穿着利落的灰西装和擦亮的皮鞋来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antiseptic</td><td>/ˌæntəˈseptɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>洁净无菌的；防腐剂</td><td>an antiseptic white room<br>洁净无菌的白色房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lab was antiseptic and almost uncomfortably tidy.  实验室洁净得几乎让人不自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apathetic</td><td>/ˌæpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无兴趣的</td><td>apathetic voters<br>冷漠的选民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most students seemed apathetic about the election and did not vote.  大多数学生对这次选举漠不关心，也没有投票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brittle</td><td>/ˈbrɪtl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易碎的，脆弱的；冷淡的，不真心的</td><td>brittle old paper<br>脆化的旧纸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brittle old pages cracked when I turned them carefully.  我小心翻动时，那些脆化的旧书页还是裂开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chilly</td><td>/ˈtʃɪli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冷的；冷淡的</td><td>a chilly reception<br>冷淡的接待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal met a chilly reception from the committee.  这项提议受到委员会的冷淡对待。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cold-blooded</td><td>/ˌkoʊldˈblʌdɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷血的，无感情的</td><td>a cold-blooded killer<br>冷血的杀手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court described the crime as cold-blooded and carefully planned.  法庭形容这起罪行冷血且经过精心策划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frozen</td><td>/ˈfroʊzən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冻结的，冰冻的</td><td>a frozen lake<br>结冰的湖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children skated across the frozen pond behind the church.  孩子们在教堂后结冰的池塘上滑冰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indifferent</td><td>/ɪnˈdɪfrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感兴趣的，冷漠的；平庸的</td><td>indifferent to criticism<br>对批评漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is entirely indifferent to what her critics say.  她对批评者说什么完全无所谓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfriendly</td><td>/ʌnˈfrendli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不友好的，冷淡的</td><td>an unfriendly stare<br>不友好的注视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waiter gave us an unfriendly look and walked away.  服务员不友好地看了我们一眼便走开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsympathetic</td><td>/ˌʌnsɪmpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不同情的，冷漠的</td><td>an unsympathetic audience<br>冷漠的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was unsympathetic to complaints about long hours.  经理对加班时间过长的抱怨毫无同情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wintry</td><td>/ˈwɪntri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冬般的；冷淡的</td><td>a wintry smile<br>冷淡的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted the visitors with a wintry nod.  她只对来客冷淡地点了点头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ardent</td><td>/ˈɑːrdənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，热切的</td><td>an ardent supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an ardent fan of the team for fifty years.  他五十年来一直是这支球队的狂热球迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blazing</td><td>/ˈbleɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>灼热的，燃烧的</td><td>the blazing summer sun<br>灼热的夏日阳光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They worked in the blazing heat until noon.  他们在灼热的高温下干到中午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 burning</td><td>/ˈbɜːrnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迫切的；燃烧的</td><td>a burning question<br>迫切的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Housing remains the burning issue in this crowded district.  住房仍是这个拥挤城区最迫切的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fervent</td><td>/ˈfɜːrvənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满感情的，热情洋溢的</td><td>a fervent believer<br>热切的信徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a fervent believer in free public libraries for everyone.  她热切地相信应该为所有人提供免费的公共图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fervid</td><td>/ˈfɜːrvɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的</td><td>a fervid supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fervid speech moved even the quietest people in the room.  他热情洋溢的演讲连房间里最安静的人都被感动了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 molten</td><td>/ˈmoʊltən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熔化的，炽热的</td><td>molten steel in the furnace<br>炉中的熔钢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Molten rock poured slowly down the eastern slope.  熔岩缓缓流下东坡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 roasting</td><td>/ˈroʊstɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的，灼热的</td><td>a roasting afternoon<br>酷热的下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We crossed the desert on a roasting July afternoon.  我们在七月一个酷热的下午穿过沙漠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scalding</td><td>/ˈskɔːldɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>滚烫的；（言辞）严厉的</td><td>scalding criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic wrote a scalding review of the opening night.  评论家为首演之夜写了一篇严厉的评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scorching</td><td>/ˈskɔːrtʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的，灼热的</td><td>a scorching summer day<br>酷热的夏日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They crossed the desert under a scorching midday sun.  他们在酷热的正午阳光下穿越沙漠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 searing</td><td>/ˈsɪrɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>灼热的；尖锐的</td><td>searing heat<br>灼热的高温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The searing heat of the furnace could be felt outside.  炉子的灼热在外面都能感觉到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sultry</td><td>/ˈsʌltri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>闷热的；性感撩人的</td><td>a sultry evening<br>闷热的夜晚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sultry air made sleeping almost impossible that night.  闷热的空气让那晚几乎无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sweltering</td><td>/ˈsweltərɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的</td><td>a sweltering afternoon<br>酷热的午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We stayed indoors through the sweltering afternoon with the fans running.  酷热的午后我们开着风扇待在屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torrid</td><td>/ˈtɔːrɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的，情感深厚的</td><td>the torrid zone<br>热带地区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The torrid afternoon heat kept everyone indoors until the sun finally set.  酷热的午后让所有人待在室内，直到太阳终于落下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cordial</td><td>/ˈkɔːrdʒəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情友好的；令人振奋的</td><td>a cordial welcome<br>热情的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two leaders exchanged cordial greetings before the talks.  两位领导人在会谈前互致亲切的问候。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 friendly</td><td>/ˈfrendli/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>友好的，亲切的</td><td>a friendly welcome<br>友好的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shopkeeper was friendly and remembered every regular customer.  店主很友好，记得每位常客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genial</td><td>/ˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼亲切的；温和宜人的</td><td>a genial host<br>亲切的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our genial neighbor invited the whole street over for dinner.  我们和蔼可亲的邻居请整条街的人来吃晚饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hearty</td><td>/ˈhɑːrti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健壮的；热情的</td><td>hale and hearty<br>健壮结实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains hale and hearty.  八十岁时他依然健壮结实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sympathetic</td><td>/ˌsɪmpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同情的，有同情心的</td><td>a sympathetic listener<br>富有同情心的倾听者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse gave her a sympathetic look and held her hand.  护士同情地看着她，握住了她的手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 warm</td><td>/ˈwɔːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，亲切的</td><td>a warm welcome<br>热情的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers gave the travelers a warm welcome.  村民热情欢迎了这些旅人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 warm-blooded</td><td>/wɔrm ˈblʌdɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>温血的；热情的</td><td>warm-blooded animals<br>温血动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Birds are warm-blooded and keep a steady body temperature.  鸟类是温血动物，能保持稳定体温。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 warmhearted</td><td>/ˈwɔrm ˈhɑrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热心的，热情友善的</td><td>a warmhearted neighbor<br>一位热心的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their warmhearted host insisted that everyone stay for dinner.  他们热心的主人坚持要所有人留下吃饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>gawky</b></td><td>/ˈɡɔːki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（举止）笨拙的</td><td>a gawky teenage boy<br>笨手笨脚的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gawky teenage boy tripped over his own long legs.  那个笨手笨脚的少年被自己的长腿绊倒了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 awkward</td><td>/ˈɔːkwərd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不灵巧的；尴尬的</td><td>an awkward silence<br>尴尬的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An awkward silence followed his remark about her divorce.  他谈到她离婚之后，出现了一阵尴尬的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clumsy</td><td>/ˈklʌmzi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不灵巧的</td><td>a clumsy attempt<br>笨拙的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My clumsy attempt at juggling ended with three broken plates.  我笨拙地学抛球，结果打碎了三个盘子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 gawkish</del></td><td>/ˈɡɔkɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，呆头呆脑的</td><td>a gawkish bow<br>笨拙的鞠躬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His gawkish attempts at dancing amused the whole room.  他笨拙的跳舞尝试让满屋人发笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 graceless</td><td>/ˈɡreɪsləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不优雅的</td><td>a graceless heavy stride<br>笨重不雅的步态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His graceless attempt at an apology made things worse.  他笨拙的道歉尝试让事情更糟了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncoordinated</td><td>/ˌʌnkoʊˈɔːrdəneɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>动作不协调的</td><td>uncoordinated arm movements<br>不协调的手臂动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy was still clumsy and uncoordinated on the stairs.  小狗在楼梯上还是笨拙又不协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ungainly</td><td>/ʌnˈɡeɪnli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不雅的</td><td>an ungainly walk<br>笨拙的步态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young bird made an ungainly landing on the narrow branch.  那只幼鸟笨拙地落在窄窄的树枝上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agile</td><td>/ˈædʒəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏捷的，灵活的</td><td>an agile climber<br>敏捷的攀登者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The agile goat crossed the rocks without slipping.  那只敏捷的山羊越过岩石毫不滑倒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 graceful</td><td>/ˈɡreɪsfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>优雅的，优美的</td><td>a graceful dancer<br>姿态优雅的舞者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a graceful turn and landed without a sound.  她优雅地一个转身，落地时毫无声响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lithe</td><td>/laɪð/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏捷的，轻盈的；柔软的</td><td>a lithe young cat<br>轻盈的小猫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was lithe enough to slip through the narrow gap.  她身形轻盈，足以从窄缝中溜过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nimble</td><td>/ˈnɪmbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏捷的，灵活的</td><td>nimble fingers<br>灵活的手指</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her nimble fingers tied the knot in one second.  她灵活的手指一秒钟就打好了结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>germinate</b></td><td>/ˈdʒɜːrmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>发芽；出现</td><td>germinate in warm soil<br>在温暖土壤中发芽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These seeds germinate quickly in warm, moist soil near the window.  这些种子在窗边温暖潮湿的土壤中很快发芽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burgeon</td><td>/ˈbɜːrdʒən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>迅速成长扩大，蓬勃发展</td><td>a burgeoning market<br>迅速发展的市场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's tech sector burgeoned after the new university opened.  新大学开办后，该市的科技产业迅速壮大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shoot</td><td>/ˈʃuːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>射出，发射；迅速射出（光线等）</td><td>shoot a glance at<br>朝…投去一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lighthouse shot a beam of light across the dark water.  灯塔把一束光射过漆黑的水面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sprout</td><td>/ˈspraʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>发芽，长出</td><td>sprout after the rain<br>雨后发芽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The beans sprouted within four days of planting.  这些豆子播下四天内就发芽了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 develop</td><td>/dɪˈveləp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>发展，培养</td><td>develop a skill<br>培养一项技能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She developed a habit of reading for an hour each night.  她养成了每晚阅读一小时的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evolve</td><td>/ɪˈvɑːlv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>逐渐形成，发展</td><td>evolve into a movement<br>逐渐发展为一场运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small reading group evolved into a national society.  这个小读书会逐渐发展成全国性协会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grow</td><td>/ˈɡroʊ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>种植，生长；成长</td><td>grow vegetables<br>种蔬菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They grow tomatoes and beans on a small rooftop garden.  他们在小小的屋顶花园里种番茄和豆子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fade</td><td>/ˈfeɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>衰退，消退；褪色</td><td>hopes began to fade<br>希望开始消退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their hopes of rescue began to fade after three days.  三天后他们获救的希望开始消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flag</td><td>/flæɡ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>变得衰弱</td><td>his energy began to flag<br>他的精力开始衰减</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His energy began to flag after the twentieth mile.  跑到第二十英里后他的精力开始衰减。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wilt</td><td>/wɪlt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>精神萎靡，憔悴</td><td>wilt in the heat<br>在酷热中萎靡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The runners began to wilt in the heat of the final mile.  跑到最后一英里的酷热中，选手们开始萎靡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wither</td><td>/ˈwɪðər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>枯萎，凋谢</td><td>wither in the heat<br>在高温中枯萎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tomato plants withered after a week without rain or shade.  一周无雨又没有遮荫，番茄苗都枯萎了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disappear</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈpɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>消失，不见</td><td>disappear without trace<br>消失得无影无踪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path disappeared into thick fog halfway up.  小径在半山处消失在浓雾中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vanish</td><td>/ˈvænɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>消失，突然不见</td><td>vanish into thin air<br>凭空消失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her keys vanished somewhere between the car and the door.  她的钥匙在车与门之间某处不见了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>glacial</b></td><td>/ˈɡleɪsiəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极冷的；冷漠的，无感情的</td><td>a glacial silence<br>冰冷的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His glacial tone made everyone at the table stop talking at once.  他冰冷的语气让桌边所有人立刻停止了交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 algid</del></td><td>/ˈældʒɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冷的，冰冷的</td><td>an algid winter night<br>寒冷的冬夜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The algid wind off the lake numbed their faces within minutes.  湖面吹来的寒风几分钟就冻麻了他们的脸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arctic</td><td>/ˈɑːrktɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极寒的；北极的</td><td>arctic temperatures<br>极寒的气温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An arctic wind swept across the empty parking lot.  一阵极寒的风扫过空荡的停车场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bitter</td><td>/ˈbɪtər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严寒刺骨的；苦的</td><td>a bitter north wind<br>刺骨的北风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We waited an hour in bitter cold for the train.  我们在刺骨的严寒中等了一小时火车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chilling</td><td>/ˈtʃɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺骨的；令人恐惧的</td><td>a chilling wind<br>刺骨的寒风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A chilling drizzle soaked us before we reached shelter.  刺骨的细雨在我们到达庇护处前把我们淋透。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 coldish</del></td><td>/ˈkoʊldɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有点冷的，微寒的</td><td>a coldish spring morning<br>微寒的春晨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was coldish outside, so we took our coats.  外面有点冷，我们就带上了外套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cool</td><td>/ˈkuːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>凉爽的；冷淡的</td><td>a cool reception<br>冷淡的接待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His proposal met a cool response from the committee.  他的提案得到委员会冷淡的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 freezing</td><td>/ˈfriːzɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极冷的，冰冻的</td><td>freezing temperatures<br>冰点以下的气温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We waited on the freezing platform for half an hour.  我们在极冷的站台上等了半小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frigid</td><td>/ˈfrɪdʒɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严寒的；冷漠的</td><td>a frigid stare<br>冷冰冰的凝视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the latecomer a frigid stare and said nothing.  她冷冰冰地盯了迟到者一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frosty</td><td>/ˈfrɔːsti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冷的；无强烈感情的，冷淡的</td><td>a frosty welcome<br>冷淡的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The visitors received a frosty welcome from the village elders.  来访者受到村中长者冷淡的接待。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 gelid</del></td><td>/ˈdʒɛlɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冰冷的，极寒的</td><td>gelid mountain water<br>冰冷的山泉水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He washed his face in the gelid stream and gasped.  他在冰冷的溪水里洗脸，冷得直吸气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 icy</td><td>/ˈaɪsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冰冷的；冷淡的</td><td>an icy road<br>结冰的道路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave him an icy stare and said nothing.  她冷冰冰地瞪了他一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nippy</td><td>/ˈnɪpi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺骨的，寒冷的</td><td>a nippy autumn wind<br>刺骨的秋风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It turned nippy as soon as the sun went down.  太阳一落山天就变得刺骨地冷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 numbing</td><td>/ˈnʌmɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>使麻木的，冻得发麻的</td><td>numbing cold on deck<br>甲板上冻得发麻的严寒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The numbing wind made it hard to hold the rope.  刺骨发麻的寒风让人难以抓住绳子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 polar</td><td>/ˈpoʊlər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全对立的；极地的</td><td>polar opposites<br>截然相反的两极</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brothers are polar opposites in almost every way.  兄弟俩几乎在各方面都截然相反。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snappy</td><td>/ˈsnæpi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>时髦利落的；简洁的</td><td>a snappy jacket<br>利落时髦的外套</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in a snappy grey suit and polished shoes.  他穿着利落的灰西装和擦亮的皮鞋来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antiseptic</td><td>/ˌæntəˈseptɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>洁净无菌的；防腐剂</td><td>an antiseptic white room<br>洁净无菌的白色房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lab was antiseptic and almost uncomfortably tidy.  实验室洁净得几乎让人不自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apathetic</td><td>/ˌæpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无兴趣的</td><td>apathetic voters<br>冷漠的选民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most students seemed apathetic about the election and did not vote.  大多数学生对这次选举漠不关心，也没有投票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brittle</td><td>/ˈbrɪtl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易碎的，脆弱的；冷淡的，不真心的</td><td>brittle old paper<br>脆化的旧纸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brittle old pages cracked when I turned them carefully.  我小心翻动时，那些脆化的旧书页还是裂开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chilly</td><td>/ˈtʃɪli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冷的；冷淡的</td><td>a chilly reception<br>冷淡的接待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal met a chilly reception from the committee.  这项提议受到委员会的冷淡对待。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cold-blooded</td><td>/ˌkoʊldˈblʌdɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷血的，无感情的</td><td>a cold-blooded killer<br>冷血的杀手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court described the crime as cold-blooded and carefully planned.  法庭形容这起罪行冷血且经过精心策划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frozen</td><td>/ˈfroʊzən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冻结的，冰冻的</td><td>a frozen lake<br>结冰的湖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children skated across the frozen pond behind the church.  孩子们在教堂后结冰的池塘上滑冰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indifferent</td><td>/ɪnˈdɪfrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感兴趣的，冷漠的；平庸的</td><td>indifferent to criticism<br>对批评漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is entirely indifferent to what her critics say.  她对批评者说什么完全无所谓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfriendly</td><td>/ʌnˈfrendli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不友好的，冷淡的</td><td>an unfriendly stare<br>不友好的注视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waiter gave us an unfriendly look and walked away.  服务员不友好地看了我们一眼便走开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsympathetic</td><td>/ˌʌnsɪmpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不同情的，冷漠的</td><td>an unsympathetic audience<br>冷漠的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was unsympathetic to complaints about long hours.  经理对加班时间过长的抱怨毫无同情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wintry</td><td>/ˈwɪntri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冬般的；冷淡的</td><td>a wintry smile<br>冷淡的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted the visitors with a wintry nod.  她只对来客冷淡地点了点头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ardent</td><td>/ˈɑːrdənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，热切的</td><td>an ardent supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an ardent fan of the team for fifty years.  他五十年来一直是这支球队的狂热球迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blazing</td><td>/ˈbleɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>灼热的，燃烧的</td><td>the blazing summer sun<br>灼热的夏日阳光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They worked in the blazing heat until noon.  他们在灼热的高温下干到中午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 burning</td><td>/ˈbɜːrnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迫切的；燃烧的</td><td>a burning question<br>迫切的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Housing remains the burning issue in this crowded district.  住房仍是这个拥挤城区最迫切的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fervent</td><td>/ˈfɜːrvənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满感情的，热情洋溢的</td><td>a fervent believer<br>热切的信徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a fervent believer in free public libraries for everyone.  她热切地相信应该为所有人提供免费的公共图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fervid</td><td>/ˈfɜːrvɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的</td><td>a fervid supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fervid speech moved even the quietest people in the room.  他热情洋溢的演讲连房间里最安静的人都被感动了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 molten</td><td>/ˈmoʊltən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熔化的，炽热的</td><td>molten steel in the furnace<br>炉中的熔钢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Molten rock poured slowly down the eastern slope.  熔岩缓缓流下东坡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 roasting</td><td>/ˈroʊstɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的，灼热的</td><td>a roasting afternoon<br>酷热的下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We crossed the desert on a roasting July afternoon.  我们在七月一个酷热的下午穿过沙漠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scalding</td><td>/ˈskɔːldɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>滚烫的；（言辞）严厉的</td><td>scalding criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic wrote a scalding review of the opening night.  评论家为首演之夜写了一篇严厉的评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scorching</td><td>/ˈskɔːrtʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的，灼热的</td><td>a scorching summer day<br>酷热的夏日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They crossed the desert under a scorching midday sun.  他们在酷热的正午阳光下穿越沙漠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 searing</td><td>/ˈsɪrɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>灼热的；尖锐的</td><td>searing heat<br>灼热的高温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The searing heat of the furnace could be felt outside.  炉子的灼热在外面都能感觉到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sultry</td><td>/ˈsʌltri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>闷热的；性感撩人的</td><td>a sultry evening<br>闷热的夜晚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sultry air made sleeping almost impossible that night.  闷热的空气让那晚几乎无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sweltering</td><td>/ˈsweltərɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的</td><td>a sweltering afternoon<br>酷热的午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We stayed indoors through the sweltering afternoon with the fans running.  酷热的午后我们开着风扇待在屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torrid</td><td>/ˈtɔːrɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的，情感深厚的</td><td>the torrid zone<br>热带地区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The torrid afternoon heat kept everyone indoors until the sun finally set.  酷热的午后让所有人待在室内，直到太阳终于落下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cordial</td><td>/ˈkɔːrdʒəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情友好的；令人振奋的</td><td>a cordial welcome<br>热情的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two leaders exchanged cordial greetings before the talks.  两位领导人在会谈前互致亲切的问候。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 friendly</td><td>/ˈfrendli/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>友好的，亲切的</td><td>a friendly welcome<br>友好的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shopkeeper was friendly and remembered every regular customer.  店主很友好，记得每位常客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genial</td><td>/ˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼亲切的；温和宜人的</td><td>a genial host<br>亲切的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our genial neighbor invited the whole street over for dinner.  我们和蔼可亲的邻居请整条街的人来吃晚饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hearty</td><td>/ˈhɑːrti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健壮的；热情的</td><td>hale and hearty<br>健壮结实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains hale and hearty.  八十岁时他依然健壮结实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sympathetic</td><td>/ˌsɪmpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同情的，有同情心的</td><td>a sympathetic listener<br>富有同情心的倾听者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse gave her a sympathetic look and held her hand.  护士同情地看着她，握住了她的手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 warm</td><td>/ˈwɔːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，亲切的</td><td>a warm welcome<br>热情的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers gave the travelers a warm welcome.  村民热情欢迎了这些旅人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 warm-blooded</td><td>/wɔrm ˈblʌdɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>温血的；热情的</td><td>warm-blooded animals<br>温血动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Birds are warm-blooded and keep a steady body temperature.  鸟类是温血动物，能保持稳定体温。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 warmhearted</td><td>/ˈwɔrm ˈhɑrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热心的，热情友善的</td><td>a warmhearted neighbor<br>一位热心的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their warmhearted host insisted that everyone stay for dinner.  他们热心的主人坚持要所有人留下吃饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>glide</b></td><td>/ɡlaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>轻松地行动，轻松地通过</td><td>glide across the floor<br>轻盈地滑过地板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The skaters glided across the frozen pond without making a single sound.  滑冰者们悄无声息地轻盈滑过结冰的池塘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bowl</td><td>/ˈboʊl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>平稳快速地行进</td><td>bowl along the highway<br>在公路上飞驰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old truck bowled along the empty coastal road.  那辆旧卡车在空旷的海岸公路上平稳飞驰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breeze</td><td>/ˈbriːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻而易举的事；微风</td><td>a breeze to finish<br>轻松就能做完</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final exam was a breeze compared with the weekly quizzes.  与每周的小测相比，期末考试轻松得多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brush</td><td>/ˈbrʌʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>轻擦，掠过</td><td>brush against her sleeve<br>轻擦过她的袖子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A moth brushed past his cheek in the dark.  黑暗中一只蛾子从他脸颊边掠过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coast</td><td>/ˈkoʊst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>滑行，惯性前进</td><td>coast down the hill<br>滑行下坡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He switched off the engine and coasted into the driveway.  他关掉发动机，滑行进了车道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cruise</td><td>/ˈkruːz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓慢巡行，漫游</td><td>cruise along the coast<br>沿着海岸缓行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We cruised slowly down the river all afternoon.  我们整个下午沿河缓慢漫游。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drift</td><td>/ˈdrɪft/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>漂流，漫无目的地移动</td><td>drift from town to town<br>从一个城镇漂到另一个</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drifted through Europe for two years without any plans.  他毫无计划地在欧洲漂了两年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 roll</td><td>/ˈroʊl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>滚滚流动，翻滚</td><td>the river rolls on<br>河水滚滚向前</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The muddy river rolled past the ruined mill without stopping.  浑浊的河水滚滚流过废弃的磨坊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sail</td><td>/ˈseɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>平稳滑行，掠过</td><td>sail across the room<br>轻盈地穿过房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hawk sailed silently over the wide open field.  那只鹰无声地掠过开阔的田野。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skim</td><td>/ˈskɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>略读，浏览</td><td>skim the report quickly<br>快速浏览报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She skimmed the long report during the short taxi ride.  她在短短的出租车路程中浏览了那份长报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slide</td><td>/ˈslaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>滑行，顺畅移动；下滑</td><td>slide into the room<br>悄然滑进房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She slid into the room without making any sound.  她悄无声息地滑进了房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slip</td><td>/ˈslɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>溜走；滑倒</td><td>slip through the crowd<br>从人群中溜过</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She slipped quietly out of the meeting before the vote.  她在投票前悄悄溜出了会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stream</td><td>/ˈstriːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>流出，涌流</td><td>tears streamed down<br>泪水涌流而下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sunlight streamed through the tall windows onto the wooden floor.  阳光从高窗涌入，洒在木地板上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sweep</td><td>/ˈswiːp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扫除，清除</td><td>sweep away all opposition<br>扫除一切反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform swept away centuries of local privilege.  这项改革扫除了几百年的地方特权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 whisk</td><td>/ˈwɪsk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>迅速移动，飞快带走</td><td>whisk him away<br>飞快把他带走</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guards whisked the singer away before the crowd noticed.  在人群察觉前保镖飞快把歌手带走了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flounder</td><td>/ˈflaʊndər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>笨拙地行动，挣扎</td><td>flounder in the water<br>在水中挣扎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The swimmer floundered in the cold water before help arrived.  救援到来之前，游泳者在冷水中挣扎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 struggle</td><td>/ˈstrʌɡəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>挣扎，费力地做；斗争</td><td>struggle to keep up<br>挣扎着跟上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He struggled to keep up with the rest of the class.  他挣扎着跟上班上其他同学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>glower</b></td><td>/ˈɡlaʊər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>怒目而视</td><td>glower at an opponent<br>怒目瞪视对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach glowered at the referee but said nothing about the call.  教练怒目瞪着裁判，却没对那次判罚说什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glare</td><td>/ˈɡler/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>怒视，瞪眼</td><td>glare at the driver<br>怒视那个司机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She glared at him until he put the phone away.  她怒视着他，直到他收起手机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gloom</td><td>/ˈɡluːm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>阴沉，忧郁</td><td>an air of gloom<br>一种阴郁的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A deep gloom settled over the team after the loss.  失利后一股深深的阴郁笼罩全队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lower</td><td>/ˈloʊər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>降低，减少；较低的</td><td>lower the temperature<br>降低温度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bank lowered interest rates twice in one year.  银行一年内两次降低利率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scowl</td><td>/skaʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>皱眉（表现出不高兴）</td><td>scowl at the noise<br>对噪音皱眉不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The librarian began to scowl at the students whispering near the shelves.  图书馆员开始对书架旁窃窃私语的学生皱眉不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 beam</td><td>/ˈbiːm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>笑容满面；光束</td><td>beam with pride<br>满脸自豪地微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The father beamed with pride as his daughter walked onstage.  女儿走上台时父亲笑得满脸自豪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grin</td><td>/ɡrɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>咧嘴笑，咧嘴笑着表示</td><td>grin from ear to ear<br>笑得合不上嘴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grinned broadly when the judges announced his name as the winner.  当评委宣布他获胜时，他开心地咧嘴大笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 smile</td><td>/ˈsmaɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>微笑</td><td>smile warmly at someone<br>对某人温暖地微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She smiled at the child who had dropped his toy.  她对掉了玩具的孩子微笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>graft</b></td><td>/ɡræft/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>移植，移接</td><td>graft a branch onto stock<br>把枝条接到根株上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Surgeons grafted healthy skin onto the burned area of his left arm.  医生把健康的皮肤移植到他左臂烧伤的部位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 implant</td><td>/ˌɪmˈplænt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>植入，灌输</td><td>implant an idea<br>植入一个想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film implanted a fear of deep water in many viewers.  这部电影在许多观众心中植入了对深水的恐惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transplant</td><td>/trænsˈplænt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>移植，移种</td><td>transplant the young seedlings<br>移栽幼苗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We transplanted the roses to a sunnier corner.  我们把玫瑰移栽到更向阳的角落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>grit</b></td><td>/ɡrɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（面对困难时所表现出来的）毅力</td><td>sheer grit and patience<br>纯粹的毅力与耐心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took real grit to finish the race on an injured ankle.  脚踝受伤还跑完全程，靠的是真正的毅力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 backbone</td><td>/ˈbækˌboʊn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骨气，勇气；脊骨</td><td>show some backbone<br>表现出些骨气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took real backbone to challenge his own boss publicly.  公开质疑自己的老板需要真正的骨气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constancy</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstənsi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>坚定不移，忠贞</td><td>constancy of purpose<br>目标的坚定不移</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her constancy through those hard years astonished everyone.  她在那些艰难岁月中的坚定令所有人惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fiber</td><td>/ˈfaɪbər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>骨气，品格韧性</td><td>moral fiber<br>道德骨气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crisis revealed the moral fiber of the whole crew.  这场危机显出全体船员的道德骨气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grittiness</td><td>/ˈɡrɪtinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>坚韧，有毅力</td><td>admired for her grittiness<br>因坚韧受人敬佩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team's grittiness carried them through a brutal season.  这支队伍的坚韧让他们度过了残酷的赛季。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 guts</td><td>/ɡʌts/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>勇气，胆量；内脏</td><td>have the guts<br>有胆量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had the guts to quit and start her own company.  她有胆量辞职创办自己的公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intestinal fortitude</td><td>/ɪnˈtestɪnəl ˈfɔːrtɪtuːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>勇气，胆量</td><td>show real intestinal fortitude<br>显出真正的胆量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Finishing that race took genuine intestinal fortitude.  跑完那场比赛需要真正的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pluck</td><td>/plʌk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>敢于面对困难的勇气</td><td>show great pluck<br>表现出极大勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took real pluck to speak up against the whole committee.  敢于当众反对整个委员会需要真正的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spunk</td><td>/spʌŋk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>勇气，胆量</td><td>have the spunk to refuse<br>有胆量拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took real spunk to speak against her own boss.  当面反对自己的老板需要真正的胆量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 cowardliness</del></td><td>/ˈkaʊərdlinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>怯懦，胆小</td><td>accused of cowardliness<br>被指怯懦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His silence was later condemned as sheer cowardliness.  他的沉默后来被谴责为纯粹的怯懦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 cravenness</del></td><td>/ˈkreɪvənnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极度怯懦，胆小</td><td>sheer cravenness<br>十足的懦弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His cravenness under fire shocked the men who once admired him.  他在炮火下的懦弱令曾敬佩他的人震惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 gutlessness</del></td><td>/ˈɡʌtləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>没胆量，懦弱</td><td>political gutlessness<br>政治上的懦弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters punished the senator for his gutlessness on the crucial vote.  选民因参议员在关键投票上的懦弱而惩罚了他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 pusillanimity</del></td><td>/ˌpjuːsələˈnɪməti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>胆小，懦弱无志气</td><td>diplomatic pusillanimity<br>外交上的软弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their pusillanimity allowed the aggressor to seize the border towns unopposed.  他们的懦弱使侵略者不受阻碍地占领了边境城镇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 spinelessness</del></td><td>/ˈspaɪnləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>没骨气，软弱</td><td>moral spinelessness<br>道德上的软弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His spinelessness in meetings meant unfair decisions were never challenged.  他在会上没骨气，导致不公的决定从未被质疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>groggy</b></td><td>/ˈɡrɑːɡi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的，（走路）不稳的</td><td>feel groggy after surgery<br>术后感到虚弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was still groggy and unsteady when the nurse helped him stand.  护士扶他站起来时，他仍然虚弱得站不稳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rickety</td><td>/ˈrɪkəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不稳的</td><td>a rickety wooden chair<br>摇摇晃晃的木椅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We climbed a rickety wooden staircase to reach the attic bedroom.  我们爬上摇摇晃晃的木楼梯，来到阁楼卧室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rocky</td><td>/ˈrɑːki/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>摇晃的，不稳的</td><td>a rocky old chair<br>摇晃的旧椅子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt rocky on his feet after the long flight.  长途飞行后他站着腿都发软。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shaky</td><td>/ˈʃeɪki/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可靠的，不稳的</td><td>shaky reasoning<br>站不住脚的推理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The theory rests on shaky historical evidence.  该理论建立在不可靠的历史证据上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbalanced</td><td>/ʌnˈbælənst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不平衡的；失衡的</td><td>an unbalanced diet<br>不均衡的饮食</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report gave an unbalanced picture of the city's economy.  这份报告对该市经济的描述有失均衡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsound</td><td>/ənˈsaʊnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可靠的，有缺陷的</td><td>unsound reasoning<br>有缺陷的推理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer judged the old bridge structurally unsound.  工程师判定这座旧桥结构不牢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unstable</td><td>/ʌnˈsteɪbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不稳定的，易变的</td><td>an unstable currency<br>不稳定的货币</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ladder felt unstable, so we refused to climb any higher.  梯子感觉不稳，我们不肯再往上爬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsteady</td><td>/ʌnˈstedi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不稳的，摇晃的</td><td>unsteady hands<br>不稳的手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice was unsteady as she read the letter to us.  她给我们读那封信时声音颤抖不稳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wobbly</td><td>/ˈwɑːbəli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>摇晃的，站不稳的</td><td>wobbly on her legs<br>两腿站不稳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The table is wobbly because one leg is shorter.  这张桌子晃是因为有条腿短了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 balanced</td><td>/ˈbælənst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>均衡的，协调的</td><td>a balanced diet<br>均衡的饮食</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room felt calm because the colors were carefully balanced.  房间给人平静感，因为色彩经过精心搭配。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stable</td><td>/ˈsteɪbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>稳定的；马厩</td><td>a stable income<br>稳定的收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His condition has been stable since the operation last Tuesday.  自上周二手术以来他的病情一直稳定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 steady</td><td>/ˈstedi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稳定的，持续不变的</td><td>a steady stream of visitors<br>络绎不绝的来访者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales have shown steady growth for eight straight quarters.  销售额已连续八个季度稳定增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sound</td><td>/saʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>牢固的；健康强壮的；（逻辑）严谨的</td><td>sound financial advice<br>稳妥的理财建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lawyer gave sound financial advice that saved the family thousands.  她的律师给出稳妥的理财建议，为家里省下数千元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>grudge</b></td><td>/ɡrʌdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>怨恨，仇恨</td><td>hold a grudge<br>心怀怨恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She held a grudge against her cousin for more than ten years.  她对表姐怀恨在心十多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animosity</td><td>/ˌænəˈmɑːsəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌意，憎恶</td><td>deep animosity between them<br>他们之间的深仇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of competition bred real animosity between the two families.  多年的竞争在两家之间催生了真正的敌意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animus</td><td>/ˈænɪməs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌意</td><td>personal animus against him<br>对他的个人敌意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His criticism seemed driven by personal animus rather than honest doubt.  他的批评似乎出于个人敌意，而非真诚的质疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antagonism</td><td>/ænˈtæɡənɪzəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>敌对，对抗</td><td>open antagonism toward reform<br>对改革的公开敌视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was open antagonism between the manager and his staff.  经理与员工之间存在公开的敌对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antipathy</td><td>/ænˈtɪpəθi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，反感</td><td>mutual antipathy<br>相互反感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There has long been an antipathy between the two departments.  这两个部门之间长期存在反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bitterness</td><td>/ˈbɪtərnəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>怨恨；苦味</td><td>lingering bitterness<br>挥之不去的怨恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old bitterness surfaced whenever the two brothers discussed money.  两兄弟一谈钱，旧怨就浮上来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gall</td><td>/ɡɔːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（使）焦躁，激怒</td><td>galled by the remark<br>被那句话惹恼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His smug tone galled everyone sitting around the conference table.  他自鸣得意的语气激怒了围坐在会议桌旁的每个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hostility</td><td>/hɑːˈstɪləti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌意，敌对</td><td>open hostility toward<br>对…公开的敌意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan met with open hostility from local residents.  该计划遭到当地居民的公开敌视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jaundice</td><td>/ˈdʒɔːndɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（因嫉妒或厌世而产生的）偏见</td><td>view with jaundice<br>带偏见看待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long unemployment had filled him with jaundice toward everyone still working.  长期失业使他对所有还有工作的人心生偏见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 malice</td><td>/ˈmæləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>恶意，怨恨</td><td>bear him no malice<br>对他并无恶意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She insisted the remark carried no malice at all.  她坚称那句话完全没有恶意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rancor</td><td>/ˈræŋkər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌意，深仇</td><td>speak without rancor<br>不带怨恨地说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She described the long dispute without any rancor toward her opponents.  她讲述这场长期争端时对对手毫无怨恨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resentment</td><td>/rɪˈzentmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>怨恨，愤恨</td><td>harbor deep resentment<br>心怀深深怨恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of unequal pay bred quiet resentment among staff.  多年薪酬不公在员工中滋生了无声的怨恨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amity</td><td>/ˈæməti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>友好关系，亲善和睦</td><td>a treaty of amity<br>友好条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two nations signed a treaty of amity and commerce.  两国签署了友好通商条约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>grumble</b></td><td>/ˈɡrʌmbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（尤指低声地）抱怨，埋怨</td><td>grumble under one's breath<br>低声抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers grumbled about the new rules but followed them anyway.  工人们低声抱怨新规定，但还是照办了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carp</td><td>/kɑːrp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>对小事吹毛求疵，挑剔，表现不满</td><td>carp at every detail<br>对每个细节都挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She carped at every detail of the seating arrangement.  她对座位安排的每个细节都挑剔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 croak</td><td>/ˈkroʊk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨，发牢骚；粗哑地叫</td><td>croak about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He croaked about the food from the moment we sat down.  我们一坐下他就开始抱怨饭菜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fuss</td><td>/fʌs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>小题大做，抱怨；忙乱</td><td>make a fuss about<br>为…大惊小怪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't make a fuss about a tiny stain on the tablecloth.  别为桌布上一小块污渍小题大做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gripe</td><td>/ɡraɪp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨</td><td>gripe about the food<br>抱怨饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The passengers griped loudly about the delay for nearly a whole hour.  乘客们大声抱怨延误，抱怨了将近一个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grouch</td><td>/ˈɡraʊtʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>抱怨；牢骚满腹的人</td><td>grouch about the rules<br>抱怨规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grouched about the new rules but followed them anyway.  他抱怨新规定，但还是照做了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grouse</td><td>/ˈɡraʊs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>grouse about long hours<br>抱怨工时太长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crew groused about long hours during the harvest season.  收割季里工人们抱怨工时太长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grump</td><td>/ˈɡrʌmp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>生闷气；脾气坏的人</td><td>grump about the weather<br>对天气发牢骚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grumped about the weather all the way to the station.  他一路到车站都在抱怨天气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moan</td><td>/ˈmoʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>哀叹，抱怨</td><td>moan about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moaned about the heat for the entire drive.  整段车程他们都在抱怨炎热。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 murmur</td><td>/ˈmɜːrmər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>murmur a complaint<br>低声抱怨一句</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers murmured about the new rules but nobody complained openly.  工人们对新规定低声抱怨，却没人公开抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mutter</td><td>/ˈmʌtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨</td><td>mutter under one's breath<br>低声嘟囔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He muttered under his breath as he carried the heavy boxes upstairs.  他把沉重的箱子搬上楼时低声嘟囔着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 repine</del></td><td>/rɪˈpaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨，表达不满；渴望</td><td>repine at one's fate<br>抱怨自己的命运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rather than repine at his losses, he quietly started the business again.  他没有抱怨自己的损失，而是默默重新开始了生意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 crow</td><td>/kroʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>得意地夸耀，自鸣得意</td><td>crow about the win<br>为获胜而得意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He crowed about his victory until even his closest friends grew tired.  他为胜利得意个不停，连最亲近的朋友都听厌了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rejoice</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欣喜，喜悦</td><td>rejoice at the news<br>为消息而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced at the news of the safe return.  听到平安归来的消息，全村都欣喜不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>halfhearted</b></td><td>/ˌhæfˈhɑːrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感兴趣的，不热情的</td><td>a halfhearted attempt<br>不情不愿的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His halfhearted apology satisfied nobody in the crowded room.  他不情不愿的道歉没让挤满一屋的人满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lukewarm</td><td>/ˈluːkwɔːrm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不热情的；温热的</td><td>a lukewarm response<br>冷淡的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal met a lukewarm response from the whole committee.  这项提案在整个委员会得到冷淡的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tepid</td><td>/ˈtepɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不太热心的</td><td>a tepid response<br>不热情的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal met a tepid response from the board members.  提案得到董事们不热情的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 uneager</del></td><td>/ʌnˈiːɡər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不热切的，不情愿的</td><td>uneager to volunteer<br>不愿主动报名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team was oddly uneager to take on the new project.  团队对接手新项目出奇地不积极。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unenthusiastic</td><td>/ˌʌnɪnθuːziˈæstɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不热情的，兴趣不大的</td><td>an unenthusiastic response<br>反应冷淡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unenthusiastic nod told us he disliked the idea.  他不情不愿的点头说明他不喜欢这个主意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eager</td><td>/ˈiːɡər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>渴望的，热切的</td><td>eager to learn<br>渴望学习</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were eager to open their presents at once.  孩子们急切地想立刻打开礼物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enthusiastic</td><td>/ɪnˌθuːziˈæstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，热心的</td><td>enthusiastic about the plan<br>对该计划很热心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students were enthusiastic about the field trip.  学生们对这次实地考察很热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 keen</td><td>/ˈkiːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏锐的，锐利的</td><td>a keen eye for detail<br>对细节的敏锐眼光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photographer has a keen eye for unusual light.  这位摄影师对特别的光线有敏锐的眼力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 passionate</td><td>/ˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，充满激情的</td><td>a passionate defense of art<br>对艺术的热情辩护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is passionate about protecting the city's old neighborhoods.  她对保护城市的老街区充满热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 warm</td><td>/ˈwɔːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，亲切的</td><td>a warm welcome<br>热情的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers gave the travelers a warm welcome.  村民热情欢迎了这些旅人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>heckle</b></td><td>/ˈhekəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>起哄，使难堪</td><td>heckle the speaker<br>向演讲者起哄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Protesters began to heckle the speaker throughout the entire rally.  抗议者在整场集会上不断起哄质问演讲者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 badger</td><td>/ˈbædʒər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>纠缠，一再烦扰</td><td>badger someone into agreeing<br>纠缠迫使某人同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters badgered the coach into revealing his starting lineup.  记者们不断纠缠教练，逼他透露首发阵容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bait</td><td>/beɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>故意惹恼，戏弄；引诱</td><td>bait him about his accent<br>拿他的口音戏弄他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The older boys baited him about his accent until he cried.  大孩子们拿他的口音戏弄他，直到他哭了出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hassle</td><td>/ˈhæsəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>争吵；麻烦事</td><td>a hassle over money<br>为钱的一场争吵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They had a long hassle over who should pay.  他们为该谁付钱争吵了很久。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 haze</td><td>/ˈheɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>欺侮，戏弄（新人）</td><td>haze the new recruits<br>戏弄新兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Older members used to haze the new recruits for a whole week.  老成员过去会整整一周戏弄新来的成员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hector</td><td>/ˈhektər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>威吓，欺凌恫吓</td><td>hector the staff<br>恫吓下属</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach hectored his players throughout the second half.  整个下半场教练都在对球员大声恫吓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 needle</td><td>/ˈniːdəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不断刺激，挑逗嘲弄</td><td>needle someone about failure<br>拿失败挖苦别人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His brothers needled him about the lost game for months.  他的兄弟们拿那场败局挖苦他好几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ride</td><td>/ˈraɪd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不断嘲弄，苛责</td><td>ride someone hard<br>对某人不停挑刺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editors sometimes ride young reporters about every comma in their first stories.  编辑有时会为新记者初稿里的每个逗号不停挑刺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taunt</td><td>/tɔːnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>嘲弄，讥讽；挑衅</td><td>taunt the loser<br>嘲弄失败者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The winning boys taunted the loser until a teacher intervened.  获胜的男孩们不停嘲弄输者，直到老师出面干预。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>hierarchical</b></td><td>/ˌhaɪəˈrɑːrkɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>分等级的，等级制的</td><td>a rigid hierarchical structure<br>僵化的等级结构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company's hierarchical structure discouraged junior staff from speaking up.  公司的等级制结构使低层员工不敢发声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 graded</td><td>/ˈɡreɪdəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有坡度的，分级的</td><td>a gently graded slope<br>缓缓倾斜的坡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path is graded so wheelchairs can climb it.  这条路有坡度设计，轮椅可以上去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 graduated</td><td>/ˈɡrædʒueɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>分级的，分等的</td><td>a graduated tax rate<br>分级税率</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country uses a graduated scale of income tax.  该国采用分级的所得税税率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ranked</td><td>/ˈræŋkt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>分等级排列的</td><td>a ranked list of schools<br>按等级排列的学校名单</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Players are seeded from a ranked list of results.  选手按成绩排名名单来定种子位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>hobble</b></td><td>/ˈhɑːbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>hobble the recovery effort<br>妨碍复苏的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor infrastructure continues to hobble economic growth in the whole region.  基础设施薄弱继续阻碍整个地区的经济增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encumber</td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍；妨碍</td><td>encumber the project<br>妨碍这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too many rules encumber small businesses in this country.  太多规定阻碍了这个国家的小企业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fetter</td><td>/ˈfetər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚</td><td>the fetters of tradition<br>传统的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy family obligations fettered her ambition for many long years.  沉重的家庭责任多年来束缚了她的雄心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 handicap</td><td>/ˈhændikæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>妨碍，使不利；障碍</td><td>handicap the team<br>使球队处于不利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor equipment handicapped the researchers from the very start.  设备不足从一开始就妨碍了研究人员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hamper</td><td>/ˈhæmpər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍；阻碍；束缚</td><td>hamper the rescue effort<br>妨碍救援工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow hampered the rescue teams trying to reach the stranded climbers.  大雪妨碍了试图接近被困登山者的救援队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hinder</td><td>/ˈhɪndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>hinder progress<br>妨碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor lighting hindered the search for survivors.  照明不足妨碍了对生还者的搜寻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impede</td><td>/ɪmˈpiːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻碍</td><td>impede progress<br>阻碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick fog impeded the rescue team's progress.  浓雾阻碍了救援队的前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inhibit</td><td>/ɪnˈhɪbɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，阻碍</td><td>inhibit the growth of bacteria<br>抑制细菌生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold weather inhibits the growth of these tropical plants.  寒冷天气抑制了这些热带植物的生长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 manacle</del></td><td>/ˈmænəkl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给…戴上手铐；束缚，限制</td><td>manacle the prisoner<br>给囚犯戴上手铐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy debts manacle the young couple, leaving them no freedom to change careers.  沉重的债务束缚了这对年轻夫妇，让他们没有转行的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstruct</td><td>/əbˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻塞</td><td>obstruct justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fallen tree obstructed the only road out of town.  一棵倒下的树堵住了唯一的出城道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shackle</td><td>/ˈʃækl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>束缚；限制，阻碍</td><td>shackle free trade<br>束缚自由贸易</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New tariffs would shackle trade between the two neighboring countries.  新关税会束缚这两个邻国之间的贸易。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stymie</td><td>/ˈstaɪmi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>stymie an investigation<br>阻碍调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Missing records stymied the investigation for months.  档案缺失使调查停滞了数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trammel</td><td>/ˈtræməl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚，限制</td><td>the trammels of custom<br>习俗的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old rules trammel the creativity of young designers here.  旧规矩束缚了这里年轻设计师的创造力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aid</td><td>/ˈeɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>帮助，援助</td><td>come to someone's aid<br>前来帮助某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors came to her aid within minutes of the fire.  火灾发生几分钟内邻居们就来帮忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assist</td><td>/əˈsɪst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，协助</td><td>assist with the work<br>协助工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two nurses assisted the surgeon during the operation.  两名护士在手术中协助医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 facilitate</td><td>/fəˈsɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变容易，促进</td><td>facilitate the discussion<br>促进讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new airport will facilitate tourism throughout the whole region.  新机场将促进整个地区的旅游业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>humdrum</b></td><td>/ˈhʌmdrʌm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调乏味的；平淡的</td><td>a humdrum routine<br>单调乏味的日常</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She left the humdrum job to travel and write full time.  她辞掉那份单调乏味的工作，去全职旅行和写作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arid</td><td>/ˈærɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>枯燥无趣的；干燥的</td><td>an arid lecture<br>枯燥的讲座</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor's arid lecture emptied half the seats before the break.  这位教授枯燥乏味的讲座在课间前就空了一半座位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drab</td><td>/dræb/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调的，无聊的</td><td>drab gray walls<br>单调的灰墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They painted over the drab gray walls with something warmer and brighter.  他们把单调的灰墙涂上了更暖更亮的颜色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dreary</td><td>/ˈdrɪri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调乏味的</td><td>a dreary afternoon<br>沉闷的下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a dreary afternoon indoors watching rain streak the windows.  我们在室内度过一个沉闷的下午，看着雨水划过窗玻璃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 drudging</del></td><td>/ˈdrʌdʒɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>苦干的，单调乏味的</td><td>drudging office work<br>枯燥的办公室工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of drudging clerical work dulled his early ambition.  多年枯燥的文书苦工磨钝了他早年的雄心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 Jading</del></td><td>/ˈdʒeɪdɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌倦的，使人生厌</td><td>a jading routine<br>令人厌倦的日常</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of jading paperwork dulled his early enthusiasm.  多年令人厌倦的文书工作磨掉了他早年的热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jejune</td><td>/dʒɪˈdʒuːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣乏味的</td><td>a jejune argument<br>乏味浅薄的论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics dismissed the sequel as jejune and repetitive.  评论家认为这部续集乏味且重复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monochromatic</td><td>/ˌmɑːnəkroʊˈmætɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单色的</td><td>a monochromatic palette<br>单色的配色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The designer chose a monochromatic scheme of soft grays.  设计师选了柔灰的单色方案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monotonous</td><td>/məˈnɑːtənəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调的，无变化的</td><td>a monotonous voice<br>单调的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His monotonous voice made the afternoon lecture almost impossible to sit through.  他单调的声音让下午的讲座几乎难以听完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pedestrian</td><td>/pəˈdestriən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平庸无奇的，令人厌倦的</td><td>a pedestrian style<br>平庸的文风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reviewer called the plot pedestrian and the dialogue painfully flat.  评论者称其情节平庸，对话乏味得令人难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ponderous</td><td>/ˈpɑːndərəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉重的</td><td>ponderous machinery<br>笨重的机器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ponderous machinery filled the workshop and shook the floor all day.  笨重的机器占满车间，整天震动地板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stale</td><td>/steɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈旧乏味的</td><td>stale jokes<br>老掉牙的笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His stale jokes made the audience shift restlessly in their seats.  他老掉牙的笑话让观众在座位上不安地挪动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stodgy</td><td>/ˈstɑːdʒi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古板守旧的，乏味的</td><td>a stodgy old textbook<br>一本乏味的旧教材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The course relied on a stodgy old textbook nobody enjoyed reading.  这门课用的是一本没人爱读的乏味旧教材。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tedious</td><td>/ˈtiːdiəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长乏味的</td><td>a tedious explanation<br>冗长乏味的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager gave a tedious explanation of the new filing system.  经理对新的归档系统作了冗长乏味的解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tiresome</td><td>/ˈtaɪərsəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌烦的</td><td>a tiresome chore<br>烦人的杂事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Washing dishes by hand is a tiresome nightly chore.  手洗碗碟是每晚烦人的活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wearisome</td><td>/ˈwɪrisəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌倦的，乏味的</td><td>a wearisome journey<br>令人疲乏的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wearisome journey took three days by slow train.  这段疲乏的旅程坐慢车要三天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absorbing</td><td>/əbˈzɔːrbɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，吸引人的</td><td>an absorbing novel<br>引人入胜的小说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I found her account of the voyage utterly absorbing.  我觉得她对航行的叙述极其引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 engaging</td><td>/ɪnˈɡeɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有吸引力的，讨人喜欢的</td><td>an engaging personality<br>讨人喜欢的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His engaging manner made strangers feel comfortable within minutes.  他讨人喜欢的举止让陌生人几分钟内就放松下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 engrossing</td><td>/ɪnˈɡroʊsɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，令人专注的</td><td>an engrossing story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary was so engrossing that nobody left early.  这部纪录片如此引人入胜，没人提早离场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gripping</td><td>/ˈɡrɪpɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>扣人心弦的，紧张的</td><td>a gripping thriller<br>扣人心弦的惊悚片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final chapter is gripping from start to finish.  最后一章从头到尾都扣人心弦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 interesting</td><td>/ˈɪntrəstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有趣的，引人入胜的</td><td>an interesting proposal<br>一个有趣的提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an interesting objection nobody had anticipated.  她提出了一个无人预料到的有趣反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intriguing</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtriːɡɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人好奇的，有趣的</td><td>an intriguing question<br>引人好奇的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an intriguing point that nobody had considered.  她提出了一个无人考虑过的有趣观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 involving</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlvɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，扣人心弦的</td><td>an involving story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary is more involving than any thriller this year.  这部纪录片比今年任何惊悚片都更扣人心弦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 riveting</td><td>/ˈrɪvɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吸引人的，极迷人的</td><td>a riveting account<br>引人入胜的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary offers a riveting account of life inside the mountain villages.  这部纪录片对山村生活的叙述引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 thrilling</td><td>/ˈθrɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>激动人心的，令人兴奋的</td><td>a thrilling final match<br>激动人心的决赛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The last ten minutes of the game were absolutely thrilling.  比赛最后十分钟实在令人兴奋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>humiliate</b></td><td>/hjuːˈmɪlieɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>羞辱，使丧失尊严</td><td>humiliate him in public<br>当众羞辱他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher never humiliated a student for asking a simple question.  这位老师从不因学生问简单的问题而羞辱他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abase</td><td>/əˈbeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低（地位、威望、尊严）</td><td>abase oneself before someone<br>在某人面前自贬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would not abase himself by begging for the job.  他不愿低声下气地乞求这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chasten</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪsən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使谦卑，磨炼</td><td>chastened by the defeat<br>被失败磨去傲气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The public failure chastened him more than any lecture.  这次公开失败比任何说教都更让他收敛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cheapen</td><td>/ˈtʃiːpən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低价值；贬低</td><td>cheapen the brand<br>损害品牌价值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Endless discounts cheapened the label's carefully built reputation.  没完没了的折扣贬损了该品牌精心建立的声誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debase</td><td>/dɪˈbeɪs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，贬损</td><td>debase the currency<br>使货币贬值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Politicians who lie to win votes debase the office they hold.  为拉票而撒谎的政客贬损了他们所担任的职务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demean</td><td>/dɪˈmiːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，降低尊严</td><td>demean oneself<br>自贬身份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to demean his colleagues in front of clients.  他拒绝在客户面前贬低同事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discredit</td><td>/dɪsˈkredɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使丧失名誉；怀疑</td><td>discredit a witness<br>质疑证人可信度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer tried to discredit the witness with old records.  律师试图用旧档案质疑证人的可信度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disgrace</td><td>/dɪsˈɡreɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使丢脸，使失去名誉；耻辱</td><td>disgrace the family name<br>使家族名声受辱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal disgraced a politician once admired by everyone.  这桩丑闻让一位曾受众人敬仰的政客名誉扫地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dishonor</td><td>/dɪsˈɑːnər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使丢脸，玷污；不光彩</td><td>dishonor his memory<br>玷污他的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lying under oath would dishonor everything he had defended.  作伪证会玷污他一生所维护的一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shame</td><td>/ˈʃeɪm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使羞愧，羞辱；羞耻</td><td>shame him into silence<br>让他羞愧得沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher never tried to shame students in front of the class.  这位老师从不在全班面前羞辱学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sink</td><td>/ˈsɪŋk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下沉，下降；水槽</td><td>sink below the horizon<br>沉到地平线下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old boat began to sink soon after the storm.  暴风雨过后不久那条旧船开始下沉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 smirch</del></td><td>/smɜrtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>玷污（名誉）</td><td>smirch someone's reputation<br>玷污某人名誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rumor smirched the reputation he had built over thirty years.  谣言玷污了他三十年建立起来的名誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggrandize</td><td>/əˈɡrændaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大（权力、财富、声望）</td><td>aggrandize one's power<br>扩张自己的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor used the crisis to aggrandize his own power.  市长利用危机扩大自己的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 canonize</td><td>/ˈkænənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使神圣，封为圣徒</td><td>canonize a saint<br>封为圣徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The church voted to canonize the nun a century after her death.  教会在这位修女去世百年后决定将她封为圣徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deify</td><td>/ˈdeɪɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>神化，奉若神明</td><td>deify a leader<br>神化领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime tried to deify its founder in every textbook.  该政权试图在每本教科书里神化其创始人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 elevate</td><td>/ˈelɪveɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提升，提高；使兴奋</td><td>elevate the mind<br>提升心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good literature can elevate the mind and refine one's taste.  优秀的文学能提升心智、陶冶品味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exalt</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬；提升（地位）</td><td>exalt to the skies<br>推崇到极点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics exalt her early novels as masterpieces of the era.  评论家将她早期的小说赞为那个时代的杰作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>hypothetical</b></td><td>/ˌhaɪpəˈθetɪkl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>假设的；假设情形</td><td>a purely hypothetical case<br>纯属假设的情形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor posed a hypothetical to test how students weighed competing rights.  教授提出一个假设情形，考察学生如何权衡相互冲突的权利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assumption</td><td>/əˈsʌmpʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>自大，傲慢；假设</td><td>an air of assumption<br>自大的神气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His quiet assumption of authority annoyed the older members.  他不声不响地自居权威，惹恼了年长成员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 given</td><td>/ˈɡɪvən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假定的，特定的</td><td>at any given moment<br>在任一特定时刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For a given temperature the reaction rate stays constant.  在特定温度下反应速率保持不变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 postulate</td><td>/ˈpɑːstʃələt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>假定，假设</td><td>a basic postulate<br>一个基本假设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His theory rests on a single postulate that no one has yet disproved.  他的理论建立在一个至今无人驳倒的假设之上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 premise</td><td>/ˈpremɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>前提，假定</td><td>a false premise<br>错误的前提</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole plan rests on a false premise about demand.  整个计划建立在一个关于需求的错误前提上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
