<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presumption</td><td>/prɪˈzʌmpʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>冒失无礼；假定</td><td>forgive my presumption<br>请原谅我的冒失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was sheer presumption to speak for the whole committee.  代表整个委员会发言纯属冒失无礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presupposition</td><td>/ˌpriːsəpəˈzɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>预设，假定</td><td>an unexamined presupposition<br>未经检验的预设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her essay attacks an unexamined presupposition of modern economics.  她的文章批判了现代经济学一个未经检验的预设。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supposition</td><td>/ˌsʌpəˈzɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>猜想，推测</td><td>based on supposition<br>基于猜测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their conclusion rests on supposition rather than on hard evidence.  他们的结论基于猜测，而非确凿证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjectural</td><td>/kənˈdʒɛktʃərəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>推测的，猜测的</td><td>purely conjectural conclusions<br>纯属推测的结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account of the ancient battle remains largely conjectural.  他对那场古代战役的说法在很大程度上仍属推测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 speculative</td><td>/ˈspekjələtɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>推测的，猜测性的</td><td>highly speculative claims<br>高度推测性的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Much of the early chapter is frankly speculative.  开头一章的内容很大程度上属于推测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supposed</td><td>/səˈpoʊzd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>假定的，所谓的</td><td>the supposed benefits<br>所谓的好处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody could measure the supposed benefits of the expensive program.  没人能衡量这个昂贵项目所谓的好处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 suppositional</del></td><td>/ˌsʌpəˈzɪʃənəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假定的，推测的</td><td>a suppositional argument<br>假定性的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of his reasoning rested on suppositional premises.  他的大部分推理都建立在假定的前提上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conclusion</td><td>/kənˈkluːʒən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结束；结论</td><td>bring to a conclusion<br>使结束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trial reached its conclusion after eleven long weeks.  审判在漫长的十一周后结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consequence</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsəkwens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结果；重要性，价值</td><td>of little consequence<br>无关紧要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The full consequences of the new policy will not be clear for years.  这项新政策的全部后果多年内都不会明朗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deduction</td><td>/dɪˈdʌkʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>推论，演绎</td><td>logical deduction<br>逻辑推演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By simple deduction she knew the letter had been opened.  通过简单推论她就知道信被打开过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 induction</td><td>/ɪnˈdʌkʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>归纳，归纳推理</td><td>reason by induction<br>用归纳法推理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Science relies on induction from many careful observations.  科学依赖对大量细致观察的归纳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inference</td><td>/ˈɪnfərəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>推论，推断</td><td>draw a reasonable inference<br>作出合理推断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That conclusion is an inference, not a documented fact.  那个结论是推断，而非有据的事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>idiosyncrasy</b></td><td>/ˌɪdiəˈsɪŋkrəsi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>独特而奇怪的习惯</td><td>a harmless personal idiosyncrasy<br>无害的个人怪癖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Writing all his notes in green ink was a harmless idiosyncrasy.  用绿墨水写下所有笔记是他一个无害的怪癖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crotchet</td><td>/ˈkrɑtʃɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>怪念头，怪癖</td><td>full of odd crotchets<br>满是古怪念头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Refusing to travel on Fridays was one of his crotchets.  拒绝在周五出行是他的怪癖之一。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eccentricity</td><td>/ˌeksənˈtrɪsəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>古怪，怪癖</td><td>tolerate his eccentricity<br>容忍他的古怪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old professor's eccentricity delighted rather than annoyed students.  这位老教授的古怪让学生喜欢而非厌烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 individualism</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈvɪdʒuəlɪzəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>独特作风，个人特性</td><td>her stubborn individualism<br>她固执的独特作风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painter's individualism set her apart from every contemporary.  这位画家的独特作风让她与所有同代人不同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mannerism</td><td>/ˈmænərɪzəm/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>习惯性动作，怪癖</td><td>an odd verbal mannerism<br>奇怪的口头习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His nervous mannerisms distracted the whole audience.  他紧张的小动作让全场观众分神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oddity</td><td>/ˈɑːdəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>怪异之处；古怪的人</td><td>a local oddity<br>当地一个怪人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One oddity of the house is its doorless kitchen.  这房子的一个怪处是厨房没有门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peculiarity</td><td>/pɪˌkjuːliˈærəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>独特之处；怪癖</td><td>a peculiarity of the dialect<br>该方言的一个特点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One peculiarity of the local dialect is its heavy use of double negatives.  这种当地方言的一个特点是大量使用双重否定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quirk</td><td>/ˈkwɜːrk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>怪癖，奇特之处</td><td>an endearing little quirk<br>招人喜欢的小怪癖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Talking to his plants was just another harmless quirk.  跟植物说话只是他另一个无害的小怪癖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 singularity</td><td>/ˌsɪŋɡjəˈlærəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>独特，奇特之处</td><td>the singularity of his style<br>他风格的独特之处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics noted the singularity of her narrative voice.  评论家注意到她叙述声音的独特。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trick</td><td>/ˈtrɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>欺骗，诱骗；诡计</td><td>trick someone into paying<br>诱骗某人付钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tricked him into revealing the password to the account.  她骗他说出了账户密码。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 twist</td><td>/ˈtwɪst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>曲解，扭曲；拧</td><td>twist someone's words<br>曲解某人的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not twist my words to suit your own argument.  不要为了你自己的论点曲解我的话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conformity</td><td>/kənˈfɔːrməti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>遵从，一致，顺从</td><td>conformity to social norms<br>遵从社会规范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company demands strict conformity to its dress code.  公司要求严格遵守其着装规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sameness</td><td>/ˈseɪmnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>相同，一致；单调</td><td>a dull sameness<br>沉闷的雷同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A dull sameness marked every house on the street.  街上每栋房子都是沉闷的雷同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
